*Ji Soo Lee,Xilun Chen,Pierce Chuang,Ashish Shenoy,Jason Wei,Dohwan Ko,Hyunwoo J. Kim,Benoit Corda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文推出了WearableQA基准，包含4084个基于200名真实用户长达500天可穿戴数据的多选题，用于评估大语言模型在真实纵向健康数据上的推理能力。测试显示现有模型表现不佳（最高72.9%），证明该基准能有效区分模型能力且尚未被解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可穿戴传感技术取得了进展，但现有基准很少评估AI系统是否能对真实用户的纵向可穿戴记录进行推理。缺乏能够反映真实设备噪声、个体差异以及复杂生理关系的评估标准。

Method: 构建了包含200名用户、每人多达500天测量数据（时间序列、血液生物标志物、人口统计）的WearableQA基准。设计了16种问题类型，沿“数据推理vs健康推理”和“单信号vs跨信号推理”两个轴分类。采用“双重接地”（dual-grounding）框架，结合文献支持的生理发现与统计验证的人群生理模式来生成高质量问题。

Result: 对14个专有和开源大语言模型的评估显示，准确率范围在19.6%至72.9%之间（随机基线为10%）。大多数模型的准确率低于60%，表明该任务对当前模型而言极具挑战性。

Conclusion: WearableQA提供了一个真实且具有诊断性的基准，能够有效评估大语言模型在真实世界可穿戴数据上的推理能力，揭示了当前模型在处理纵向健康数据方面的不足。

Abstract: Recent advances in wearable sensing enable continuous monitoring of physiological and behavioral signals, yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether AI systems can reason over a real user's longitudinal wearable record. We introduce WearableQA, a benchmark comprising 4,084 10-option multiple-choice questions constructed from the wearable time series, blood biomarkers, and demographics of 200 real users, each with up to 500 days of daily measurements. WearableQA preserves authentic wearable distributions that include device noise and inter-individual variability. To evaluate distinct reasoning capabilities, we introduce 16 question types organized along two complementary axes: data versus health reasoning, which distinguishes computation over longitudinal measurements from physiological interpretation; and single- versus cross-signal reasoning, which separates reasoning about individual signals from the integration of multiple signals. To construct reliable questions at scale, we adopt a dual-grounding framework that combines literature-grounded physiological findings with statistically validated population-grounded physiological patterns. This enables the capture of meaningful relationships observed in real-world wearable data. Evaluation of 14 proprietary and open-source LLMs demonstrates that WearableQA effectively differentiates model capabilities, with performance ranging from 19.6% to 72.9% against a 10% chance baseline. Moreover, WearableQA remains far from solved: most models achieve accuracies below 60%. Overall, WearableQA provides a realistic and diagnostic benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over real-world wearable data.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Scalable Neural Video Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04273)
*Tianhao Peng,Ho Man Kwan,Fan Zhang,Shan Liu,David Bull*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了S-NVRC，一种基于隐式神经表示（INR）的可扩展视频编码框架，通过单一嵌入式比特流同时实现细粒度的码率和解码复杂度可扩展性，在UVG数据集上显著优于SHM 12.4和VTM-20.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于INR的可扩展编码方法通过增加网络层来实现可扩展性，导致码率与解码复杂度耦合，且性能无法与强力的可扩展或非可扩展编码器相媲美，因此需要一种能解耦这两者并提供高性能的新方法。

Method: 提出S-NVRC架构，采用用于特征网格的“由粗到细”前缀（coarse-to-fine prefix）来扩展码率，以及用于网络层的“嵌套”前缀（nested prefix）来扩展解码复杂度，从而从单次编码训练中支持广泛的运行点。

Result: 在UVG数据集上，S-NVRC相比SHM 12.4和多层VTM-20.0的BD-rate分别降低了43.7%和5.6%，同时提供了灵活的复杂度可扩展性。

Conclusion: S-NVRC成功实现了基于INR的视频编码中码率与解码复杂度的联合细粒度可扩展，并在压缩效率上超越了传统及现有的神经视频编码标准，具有显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Scalable video coding (SVC) encodes a video into a layered bitstream consisting of a base layer and one or multiple enhancement layers, enabling decoding at different bitrate/quality/resolution operating points to accommodate diverse device capabilities and network conditions. Due to its practical flexibility, SVC has been incorporated into major video coding standards and has recently attracted growing interest for both scene-agnostic and scene-adaptive neural video codecs. Among the latter, Implicit neural representation (INR) based codecs achieve compression by overfitting a compact neural network to an individual video, offering fast decoding and competitive coding efficiency compared to scene-agnostic neural codecs. However, research on scalable INR-based compression remains in its infancy: these methods support scalable coding by introducing additional network layers, which couple the bitrate with the decoding complexity and also cannot achieve comparable performance with strong scalable/non-scalable codecs. In this context, this paper proposes S-NVRC, a scalable INR-based video codec that jointly supports fine-grained bitrate and decoding complexity scalability from a single embedded bitstream. It adopts a coarse-to-fine prefix for feature grids and a nested prefix for network layers, which scale bitrate and decoding complexity, respectively. The proposed S-NVRC spans a wide range of bitrate and decoding-complexity using a single encoding (training) and outperforms SHM 12.4 and the multi-layer VTM-20.0, by 43.7% and 5.6% in BD-rate on the UVG dataset, while also providing flexible complexity scalability. Implemented code will be provided.

</details>


### [146] [Multi-scale Image Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04274)
*Tianhao Peng,Ho Man Kwan,Fan Zhang,Shan Liu,David Bull*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRC，一种过拟合图像编解码器，通过端到端率失真优化和多尺度表示，在CLIC2020验证集上相比VVC实现10.5%的BD-rate节省，解码复杂度在1.2至2.9 kMAC/像素之间可调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过拟合图像编解码器（如Cool-chic系列）未完全优化率失真目标：网络权重保持全精度训练，量化参数在训练后单独选择，且合成操作仅在单一尺度进行，忽略了跨尺度冗余。

Method: 提出MIRC编解码器，采用NVRC的端到端压缩流程，对所有编码组件（潜在变量、合成网络、熵模型）进行量化和熵编码，统一在率失真目标下优化；引入带跨阶段参数共享的多尺度表示以提升编码效率。

Result: 在CLIC2020专业验证集上，MIRC相比VVC（VTM 22.0）实现10.5%的BD-rate节省；提供多种配置，解码预算范围为1.2至2.9 kMAC/像素，可匹配不同部署需求。

Conclusion: MIRC通过端到端率失真优化和多尺度表示，显著提升了过拟合图像编解码器的性能，同时保持低解码复杂度，适用于不同部署场景。

Abstract: Overfitted codecs have demonstrated promising performance for image and video compression. In particular, for image compression, the Cool-chic family of models has shown competitive performance against scene-agnostic models, with orders of magnitude lower decoding complexity at the cost of a longer overfitting process. However, these overfitted image codecs are not fully optimized toward the rate-distortion objective: their network weights remain in full precision during training, and the associated quantization parameters are selected in a separate post-training stage. Furthermore, their synthesis operates at a single scale, which overlooks cross-scale redundancy. In this paper, we propose MIRC, an overfitted image codec in which every coded component, including the latents, the synthesis network, and the entropy models, is quantized and entropy coded under a single rate-distortion objective, adopting the end-to-end compression pipeline of the neural video representation codec NVRC. We further introduce a multi-scale representation with cross-stage parameter sharing, which improves coding efficiency at a small transmitted overhead. On the CLIC2020 professional validation set, MIRC achieves a 10.5% BD-rate saving against VVC (VTM 22.0). Moreover, MIRC offers a family of configurations spanning 1.2 to 2.9 kMAC per pixel, so the decoding budget can be selected to match the deployment target.

</details>


### [147] [Cross-modal triage network: a multimodal deep learning framework for severity-based triage and visual explainability in chest radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04357)
*Zinah Ghulam,Richa Mittal,Eranga Ukwatta*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态深度学习框架 CMTN，用于胸部 X 光片的严重程度分诊、病理检测和可视化解释。尽管在基准测试中表现优异，但与真实放射科医生的临床评估存在显著差异，强调了临床部署前需要放射科医生标注的真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部 X 光片数量增加导致分诊瓶颈，现有 AI 工具多为单模态二分类器，缺乏严重程度感知，且多模态系统很少与专家放射科医生进行基准对比。

Method: 提出跨模态分诊网络（CMTN），融合 Swin Transformer V2 视觉编码器和 PubMedBERT 文本编码器，通过门控交叉注意力机制进行训练。使用 MIMIC-CXR-JPG 数据集的 34,639 个图像 - 文本对，优化四阶严重程度分诊的序数焦点损失和 14 种病理的二元交叉熵损失。通过两阶段临床审计评估模型输出与专家评估的一致性。

Result: CMTN 在基准测试中表现优异（QWK=0.9341，macro-AUROC=0.9970），优于 BioViL 基线。但在盲法临床审计中与放射科医生判断的一致性显著较低（QWK=0.1399），54.3% 的热图达到临床可接受的空间定位。

Conclusion: CMTN 展示了高效的多模态架构，但算法性能与放射科医生评估的差异表明，仅基于 NLP 衍生标签的基准测试不足，临床部署前需要放射科医生标注的真实数据。

Abstract: Purpose: Increased number of chest radiograph (CXR) scans create a triage bottleneck, queueing urgent examinations behind routine ones. Existing AI tools are predominantly unimodal binary classifiers lacking severity awareness, and multimodal systems are rarely benchmarked against expert radiologists. To this end, we developed a multimodal deep learning framework for joint severity triage, pathology detection, and native visual explanation. Approach: We propose the cross-modal triage network (CMTN), fusing a Swin Transformer V2 visual encoder with a PubMedBERT text encoder via gated cross-attention. The CMTN was trained on 34,639 image-text pairs (12,489 patients) from MIMIC-CXR-JPG, optimizing an ordinal focal loss for four-tier severity triage and binary cross-entropy for 14 pathologies. Beyond quantitative benchmarking, attention heatmaps were evaluated against a blinded expert radiologist in a two-phase clinical audit comparing model triage output to expert severity assessment (100 cases) and grading spatial-semantic concordance (116 heatmaps). Results: The CMTN achieved strong ordinal agreement with reference labels (quadratic weighted kappa [QWK] = 0.9341, 95\% CI: 0.9219 to 0.9449) and macro-AUROC of 0.9970 across 14 pathologies, with 34~ms latency, outperforming the state-of-the-art BioViL multimodal baseline (QWK = 0.7679). However, the blinded Phase I clinical audit revealed substantially lower agreement with genuine radiologist judgment (QWK = 0.1399). Phase II found 54.3\% of heatmaps achieved clinically acceptable spatial localization. Conclusions: The CMTN demonstrated an efficient multimodal architecture for CXR triage. The divergence between algorithmic and radiologist agreement demonstrates that benchmark performance against NLP-derived labels is insufficient, highlighting the need for radiologist-labeled ground truth before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [148] [Ultrasound-Based Prediction of Cirrhosis Decompensation Using Large-Scale Computer Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04365)
*Guangyi Zhang,Peiyun Ni,Eugene Cheah,Rajat Chandra,Peng Guo,Raymond T. Chung,Anthony E. Samir*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于计算机视觉模型分析常规腹部超声图像的新方法，用于预测肝硬化失代偿的发生，提供了一种可扩展的风险分层和长期随访的非侵入性策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝硬化失代偿是一个关键的病程转折点，但临床医生缺乏可靠的非侵入性工具来预测其发生。现有的基于实验室的风险评分系统存在局限性，需要一种更实用、可扩展的预测方法。

Method: 研究采用大规模计算机视觉模型分析常规腹部超声图像，提取超越传统实验室风险评分的预测特征。框架整合了自动化超声数据处理与现代深度学习架构，以识别失代偿高风险患者。

Result: 该基于影像的非侵入性方法能够在临床恶化发生前识别出肝硬化失代偿高风险患者，为现有临床评分系统提供了实用的补充。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种实用的非侵入性策略，可用于肝硬化患者的风险分层和长期随访，有助于实现更早、更主动的患者管理。

Abstract: Decompensation represents a critical transition in the course of cirrhosis, yet clinicians have limited non-invasive tools to reliably predict its onset. In this study, we propose a novel imaging-based approach that leverages large-scale computer vision models to analyze routine abdominal ultrasound images and extract predictive features beyond those captured by traditional laboratory-based risk scores. Ultrasound is widely available, low cost, and suitable for longitudinal surveillance, making it an attractive modality for scalable risk stratification and long-term follow-up. Our framework integrates automated ultrasound data processing with modern deep learning architectures to identify patients at high risk of decompensation prior to the occurrence of clinical deterioration. This non-invasive strategy offers a practical complement to existing clinical scoring systems and may enable earlier, more proactive management of patients with compensated cirrhosis.

</details>


### [149] [Development and Evaluation of Ultrasound Image Learning Pipelines for MASLD Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04390)
*Guangyi Zhang,Xiaohong Wang,Eugene Cheah,Peng Guo,Brian A. Telfer,Theodore T. Pierce,Anthony E. Samir*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于B超和剪切波弹性成像（SWE）的深度学习模型，用于代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝病（MASLD）的纤维化分期和风险评估，结果显示SWE图像学习在纤维化分期中优于B超图像学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝病（MASLD）影响约30%的人群，超声成像（包括B超和SWE）广泛用于无创纤维化评估，但基于深度学习的超声图像学习在MASLD风险分层中的作用尚未充分研究。

Method: 纳入250次超声检查（每人1次），使用B超和SWE图像开发深度学习模型，采用3折交叉验证，以受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUROC）评估模型性能。

Result: 端到端SWE图像学习在纤维化分期中表现与操作者引导的SWE相当，且SWE图像学习在纤维化分期中一致优于B超图像学习：F≥2的AUROC从0.64提升至0.72，F≥3从0.67提升至0.78（p=0.02），F4从0.69提升至0.80。

Conclusion: SWE图像学习在MASLD风险分层中具有潜力，可作为无创评估工具优化纤维化分期和风险识别。

Abstract: Metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease (MASLD) affects approximately 30% of the general population. Ultrasound-based imaging, including B-mode imaging and shear wave elastography (SWE), is widely used for noninvasive fibrosis assessment; however, the role of deep learning-based ultrasound image learning for MASLD risk stratification remains insufficiently characterized. In this study, we developed and evaluated ultrasound image learning pipelines using B-mode and SWE images for fibrosis staging and identification of patients with at-risk metabolic dysfunction-associated steatohepatitis (MASH). A total of 250 ultrasound examinations, one exam per subject, were included. Model performance was evaluated using 3-fold cross-validation with area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC). End-to-end SWE image learning achieved performance comparable to operator-guided SWE across fibrosis stages. Overall, SWE-based learning consistently outperformed B-mode image learning in fibrosis staging, with AUROC improvements from 0.64 (95%CI: [0.56, 0.72]) to 0.72 (95% CI: [0.65, 0.79]) for F>=2 (significant fibrosis, p=0.11), from 0.67 (95%CI: [0.58, 0.75]) to 0.78 (95% CI:[0.72, 0.85]) for F>=3 (advanced fibrosis, p=0.02), and from 0.69 (95%CI: [0.56, 0.82]) to 0.80 (95%CI: [0.72, 0.89]) for F4 (cirrhosis, p=0.10). These findings highlight the potential of SWE image learning for MASLD risk stratification.

</details>


### [150] [Fractional-Order Adaptive Motion Magnification: Phase-Reliability Weighting for Noise-Constrained Video Amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04502)
*Alejandro Garnung Menéndez*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出FrAM（分数阶自适应运动放大）方法，通过引入分数阶导数替代恒定时间增益，并利用单信信号局部幅度作为空间权重，解决了欧拉视频放大在纹理缺失区域放大噪声的问题，实现了在保持信号放大的同时抑制背景噪声，并显著提升了处理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的欧拉视频放大技术采用统一增益，忽略了局部结构差异，导致在纹理缺失区域（单信相位不可靠时）传感器噪声与信号一同被放大，严重影响视频质量。

Method: 提出FrAM流水线，包含离线开发与因果流两个阶段：1. 时间域：用Grünwald-Letnikov分数阶导数替换恒定时间增益，实现对高频强调的连续控制；2. 空间域：用基于单信信号局部幅度的逐像素权重替换统一空间增益，以自适应地抑制噪声。

Result: 在合成的纹理与平坦区域分割序列中，FrAM在保持与欧拉基线相同放大效果的同时，将平坦区域的时间噪声维持在输入水平，且在八倍输入噪声范围内均有效。真实视频测试显示其具有更好的空间选择性和更低的背景噪声。因果化重构使每帧计算成本降低两个数量级，在640×480分辨率下达到69 fps。

Conclusion: FrAM通过分数阶微分和自适应空间加权机制，有效解决了传统方法在低纹理区域噪声放大的缺陷，同时大幅提升了算法的实时处理能力，显著改善了视频运动放大的质量与效率。

Abstract: Eulerian video amplification boosts sub-pixel motion by band-pass filtering per-pixel intensity traces and applying a uniform gain. That gain ignores local structure, so sensor noise is amplified together with the signal, especially in textureless regions where the monogenic phase is unreliable. We propose FrAM (Fractional-order Adaptive Motion Magnification), a pipeline developed first offline and then as a causal stream. It replaces the constant temporal gain with a Grünwald--Letnikov derivative of fractional order, giving continuous control over high-frequency emphasis, and replaces the uniform spatial gain with a per-pixel weight derived from the local amplitude of the monogenic signal. On a controlled synthetic sequence split into textured and flat halves, FrAM matches the amplification of the Eulerian baseline while keeping flat-region temporal noise at the input level. The reduction holds across an eightfold range of input noise levels. Real videos show improved spatial selectivity and lower background noise in every case. The causal reformulation cuts the per-frame cost by two orders of magnitude, reaching 69\,fps at 640$\times$480.

</details>


### [151] [Explainable Multimodal Deep Learning Integrating Imaging and Clinical Data for Oral Potentially Malignant Disorder Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04512)
*Ruilin You,Yihan Wang,Jiabin Chen,Cherie Wink,Petra Wilder-Smith,Rongguang Liang,Bofan Song*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: M2-OPMDNet 是一种多模态深度学习框架，结合白光/自体荧光图像与结构化临床数据，用于口腔潜在恶性病变（OPMD）检测，AUC 达 0.952，优于单模态方法，并通过 SHAP 分析验证了临床变量的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔潜在恶性病变（OPMD）是口腔癌的重要前兆，但因表型异质性高且与良性病变重叠，临床检测困难；现有基于图像的深度学习在真实场景中仅依赖视觉信息不足，需结合患者特异性风险因素。

Method: 开发 M2-OPMDNet 多模态深度学习框架，整合配准的白光与自体荧光口内图像及结构化临床信息；设计标准化问卷采集风险因素与症状；评估多种图像编码器（CNN 与基础模型架构）；使用 SHAP 分析特征与模态贡献。

Result: M2-OPMDNet 在前瞻性真实世界数据集上达到 AUC 0.952，优于单模态方法，对视觉不明显的病变更有效；SHAP 分析显示结构化临床变量显著贡献风险估计，并与图像特征互补。

Conclusion: 可解释的多模态学习结合白光/自体荧光成像与结构化临床数据，可实现准确、透明且临床可靠的 OPMD 检测；M2-OPMDNet 为真实世界口腔癌筛查与决策支持提供可扩展框架。

Abstract: Oral potentially malignant disorders (OPMDs) are critical precursors to oral cancer, yet clinical detection remains challenging because of substantial phenotypic heterogeneity and overlap with benign conditions. Although image-based deep learning shows promise for automated screening, visual information alone may be insufficient in real-world settings, where diagnostic decisions also rely on patient-specific risk factors. We developed M2-OPMDNet, a multimodal deep learning framework that integrates co-registered white-light and autofluorescence intraoral images with structured clinical information for OPMD detection. A customized questionnaire was designed to capture clinically relevant risk factors and symptoms in a standardized, reproducible format for integration with image-derived features. Multiple image encoders, including conventional convolutional neural networks and foundation model-based architectures, were evaluated using a prospectively collected dataset reflecting real-world screening conditions. Model interpretability was assessed using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) to quantify feature- and modality-level contributions. M2-OPMDNet achieved an AUC of 0.952, outperforming unimodal approaches and showing improved performance for visually subtle lesions. SHAP analysis demonstrated that structured clinical variables contributed substantially to risk estimation and complemented imaging features. These results demonstrate that explainable multimodal learning combining white-light and autofluorescence imaging with structured clinical data can provide accurate, transparent, and clinically grounded OPMD detection. M2-OPMDNet offers a scalable framework for real-world oral cancer screening and decision support.

</details>


### [152] [Cross-dataset transportability of pediatric chest X-ray deep learning across three countries: discrimination, calibration, operating-point failure, and limited-label recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05140)
*Nazim-E-Alam*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文评估了一种计算协议，分别测试了跨三个国家数据集的儿科肺炎分类AI在判别力、概率校准、固定操作点迁移、捷径信号及有限标签恢复性方面的表现，发现跨数据集迁移会导致性能显著下降，尤其是敏感性和特异性失衡，强调需单独评估各组件并量化恢复的操作负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学影像AI的外部评估往往仅关注判别力（discrimination），忽视了概率校准、操作点迁移、捷径学习及有限标签下的恢复能力等关键维度，导致模型在真实跨域场景中的可靠性被高估。

Method: 使用去重后的5,824张广州胸片训练冻结的三种子DenseNet121双视图集成模型，并在孟加拉国（BDCXR-3257）和越南（VinDr-PCXR/PediCXR）数据集上进行零样本评估；通过固定阈值测试敏感性，利用Platt校准和不同比例标签（5%-20%）进行适应性分析，并重复拟合以评估稳定性。

Result: 内部测试AUROC为0.976（敏感性95.1%），但在孟加拉国和越南数据集上AUROC降至0.798和0.742，固定阈值下敏感性骤降至6.2%和0%；使用163个标签进行Platt校准后，敏感性恢复至88.3%，但特异性仅为47.9%，警报率高达78.5%，且特异性存在显著波动。

Conclusion: 跨国数据集间的分布偏移对模型排序、概率对齐及源域定义的决策行为产生差异化影响；未来的迁移研究应分别评估这些组件，并量化表面性能恢复背后的实际操作负担。

Abstract: Background and Objective: External evaluation of medical-imaging AI is often collapsed into discrimination. We evaluated a computational protocol that separately tests discrimination, probability calibration, fixed operatingpoint transport, shortcut-associated signal, and limited-label recoverability for pediatric pneumonia classification across datasets from three countries. Methods: After exact-duplicate removal, 5,824 Guangzhou radiographs supported leakage-controlled source development and internal testing. A frozen three-seed DenseNet121 dual-view ensemble was evaluated zero-shot on BDCXR-3257 from Bangladesh (n = 3, 257) and an untouched harmonized VinDr-PCXR/PediCXR test cohort from Vietnam (n = 1, 077). Matched seed-42 variants tested architectural robustness. Secondary BDCXR analyses used a fixed 651-image adaptation pool and 2,606-image hold-out; 163, 326, and 651 labels represented 5%, 10%, and 20% of complete BDCXR. Results: Internal AUROC was 0.976 with 95.1% sensitivity. BDCXR and VinDr-PCXR AUROC were 0.798 and 0.742, while frozen-threshold sensitivity fell to 6.2% and 0%. Source-to-BDCXR AUROC degradation occurred for a full-image baseline (0.961 to 0.749), ungated dual-view model (0.977 to 0.766), and gated MixStyle model (0.966 to 0.789). With 163 BDCXR labels, Platt recalibration preserved AUROC while increasing held-out sensitivity to 88.3%, but specificity was 47.9% and the alert rate was 78.5%. Two hundred repeated 163-label fits confirmed sensitivity recovery but substantial specificity variability. Conclusions: Cross-dataset shifts across countries affected ranking, probability alignment, and source-defined decision behavior differently. Transport studies should evaluate these components separately and quantify the operational burden of apparent recovery.

</details>


### [153] [Real-World Multi-Modal and Longitudinal Lung Cancer Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05202)
*Rita Cordeiro Mendes,Maria Rita Fonseca Verdelho,Carlos Santiago,Catarina Barata*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为MMIST-LUNG的新多中心、多模态纵向肺癌数据集，包含1365名患者的多种影像、临床及基因组数据，具有真实的非均匀缺失特征。研究建立了生存预测基准，证明即使在数据严重缺失的情况下，多模态融合仍能显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态医学学习面临的两大挑战：一是缺乏能准确反映真实世界条件（如数据收集不一致、不完整）的高质量现成数据集；二是异构数据模态整合的固有难度。

Method: 构建了一个包含1365名肺癌患者的多中心纵向数据集，涵盖三种影像模态（全切片图像、CT、PET）、结构化临床数据、转录组数据及纵向随访治疗信息。该数据集特意保留了跨模态的非均匀缺失特性。在此基础上，针对12个月总生存期、疾病特异性生存期及严重缺失数据下的风险预测任务，建立了单模态与多模态基准进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，尽管数据存在高水平的缺失，整合互补模态的多模态方法在预测性能上始终优于单模态方法。

Conclusion: 该数据集成功模拟了真实的临床数据环境，验证了多模态融合在应对数据缺失和异构性时的鲁棒性与价值，为开发更可靠的临床决策支持系统提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Multi-modal learning has demonstrated strong potential in medical applications by integrating heterogeneous data sources such as medical imaging, clinical records, and genomics to improve predictive performance and support clinical decision-making. However, advances in this area are often constrained by two key challenges: the limited availability of well-curated, ready-to-use datasets that accurately reflect real-world conditions, where medical data are frequently collected inconsistently and are often incomplete; and the inherent difficulty of integrating heterogeneous data modalities. In this work, we introduce a newly curated multi-center, multi-modal, and longitudinal dataset designed to support the evaluation of a wide range of learning pipelines under realistic conditions. The dataset comprises a total of 1,365 lung cancer patients and has three imaging modalities (whole-slide images, CT scans, and PET scans), structured clinical data, transcriptomic, and longitudinal follow-up and treatment information. For each imaging modality the dataset contains more than one instance. Moreover, the dataset exhibits substantial and non-uniform missingness across modalities, making it well-suited for studying robust multi-modal fusion strategies. We further provide both uni-modal and multi-modal benchmarks on the task of 12-month overall survival prediction, disease-specific survival, as well as longitudinal benchmark of hazard prediction under severe missing data. Our results show that, despite high levels of missingness, integrating complementary modalities consistently improves predictive performance over uni-modal approaches, highlighting the value of multi-modal fusion in realistic clinical settings.
  The dataset and benchmark code are available at https://github.com/ritacmendes/MMIST-LUNG.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Minimax Lower Bound for Estimating Diffusion-based Local Intrinsic Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04822)
*Jaehee Seo,Wontae Jeong,Jisu Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于扩散的局部内在维度（LID）估计量FLIPD的统计难度，证明了在正则流形模型下，有限尺度场与真实维度$d$的偏差为$O(\sigma^2)$，并建立了估计该场的极小极大下界，揭示了其在不同尺度下的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于扩散的方法已成为探测高维数据内在几何的有效工具，但其统计难度（statistical difficulty）尚未得到充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，具体分析FLIPD这一基于扩散的局部内在维度量的底层有限尺度总体泛函的估计问题。

Method: 通过分析高斯平滑密度的对数尺度导数，利用高斯平滑将局部维度转化为尺度律（scale law）的特性（即在$d$维流形附近，核质量增长如$\sigma^d$）。在正则流形模型假设下，理论推导有限尺度场与流形维度$d$之间的偏差界限，并构建极小极大下界（minimax lower bound）以评估从$n$个观测值中估计该有限尺度场的统计难度。

Result: 1. 在模型类上一致地证明，有限尺度场与流形维度$d$的差异最多为$O(\sigma^2)$。
2. 建立了估计该有限尺度场的极小极大下界，阶数为$(n\sigma^d)^{-1}$，适用于尺度范围$n^{-1/(2\alpha+d)}\lesssim\sigma\le\sigma_0$。
3. 在下界构造所覆盖的最小尺度处，该界限退化为非参数速率$n^{-2\alpha/(2\alpha+d)}$。

Conclusion: 基于扩散的LID估计量FLIPD在理论上具有坚实的统计基础，其估计误差受尺度参数$\sigma$和样本量$n$的共同制约。研究量化了偏差与方差之间的权衡，明确了在特定尺度范围内估计内在维度的最优收敛速率，为理解扩散方法在高维数据几何探测中的统计性质提供了理论依据。

Abstract: While diffusion-based methods have recently emerged as effective tools for probing the intrinsic geometry of high-dimensional data, their statistical difficulty remains largely unexplored. We study estimation of the finite-scale population functional underlying FLIPD (Kamkari et al., 2024; arXiv:2406.03537), a diffusion-based local intrinsic dimension (LID) quantity defined through the logarithmic scale derivative of a Gaussian-smoothed density. Intuitively, Gaussian smoothing turns local dimension into a scale law: near a $d$-dimensional manifold, the kernel mass grows like $σ^d$, so differentiating with respect to the noise scale reveals the intrinsic exponent. Under a regular manifold model, we show uniformly over the model class that the finite-scale field differs from the manifold dimension $d$ by at most $O(σ^2)$. We then establish a minimax lower bound of order $(nσ^d)^{-1}$ for estimating this finite-scale field from $n$ observations, for $n^{-1/(2α+d)}\lesssimσ\leσ_0$. At the smallest scale covered by our lower-bound construction, the bound becomes the nonparametric rate $n^{-2α/(2α+d)}$.

</details>


### [155] [An Analysis of Self-supervised Pre-training with Dependent Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05031)
*Maximilian Fleissner,Debarghya Ghoshdastidar,Samory Kpotufe*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了在自监督学习中，尽管数据增强之间存在依赖性，将同一数据点的多种增强样本“池化（pooling）”在一起训练，在统计估计误差上优于或等同于将数据划分为独立子集的基线方法，且在特定条件下能实现更快的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习通常通过池化同一数据点的多种增强样本来学习不变子空间，但这引入了样本间的依赖性。现有的理论工作通常为了避免这种依赖性而仅使用独立数据子集，导致无法解释为何实践中池化大量增强样本更有效，且限制了可利用的数据规模。

Method: 通过理论分析推导统计估计误差界（statistical estimation error bounds），对比了“池化增强样本”与“划分独立数据子集”两种策略在估计不变子空间 $\cal V$ 时的表现，重点分析了增强样本间相关性对估计方差的影响。

Result: 理论证明池化策略的误差界从不劣于划分基线；在基于掩码或噪声注入的浅层神经网络等场景中，朴素池化能随增强样本数量增加获得更快的收敛速率。当增强间的相关性对估计影响较小或有助于降低估计方差时，池化的优势尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该研究为自监督预训练中池化增强样本的成功提供了理论依据，解释了实践中倾向于使用大量增强样本的直觉，表明利用依赖性数据而非规避它，在统计效率上是更优的选择。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning relies on so-called data augmentations $φ(x)$ of unlabeled datapoints $x$ --- for example, masking random pixels in an image $x$ --- that should leave the label of $x$ invariant and are often used to learn a lower-complexity invariant subspace $\cal V$ for downstream tasks. In practice, such augmentations $\{ φ_l(x_i) \}$ are pooled together to learn $\cal V$, despite obvious inter-dependencies between different augmentations $φ_l(x), φ_k(x)$ of the same datapoint $x$. However, theoretical works on the subject typically consider procedures that avoid such dependencies, and are therefore limited to operate on smaller subsets of independent data.
  We show in this work that pooling augmentations together, despite inter-dependencies, is a better alternative than the baseline of partitioning the data into subsets of independent data. More precisely, in the context of estimating $\cal V$, the statistical estimation error bounds for pooling are never worse than the partitioning baseline, and in some cases --- such as masking or noise injection-based augmentations over a shallow neural network --- naive pooling leads to faster rates in terms of the number of augmentations. The benefits of pooling are particularly prominent when the correlations between different augmentations $φ_l(x), φ_k(x)$ have mild effects on estimation or help decrease the estimation variance. The analysis, therefore, yields new insights into the success of pooling augmented samples in self-supervised pre-training, and provides an intuition behind the practical preference towards using many augmentations.

</details>


### [156] [FluxDisco: Symbolic Regression for Stoichiometric Dynamical Systems via Monte Carlo Graph Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05207)
*Cassandra Durr,Alvaro Köhn-Luque,Chris Jewell,Lloyd A. C. Chapman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出FluxDisco框架，利用化学计量学信息，通过改进的蒙特卡洛图搜索算法，从噪声数据中发现符合物理定律的通量基ODE系统控制方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准动力学符号回归方法生成的表达式常违反已知物理定律，且搜索空间过大。

Method: 提出FluxDisco物理信息框架，利用已知化学计量学约束减少搜索空间，并改进蒙特卡洛图搜索算法以适应化学计量系统的联合通量发现。

Result: 在多种物理和生物系统中评估，证明该方法能通过可解释方程准确恢复控制动力学。

Conclusion: FluxDisco有效平衡了可解释性与预测精度，确保发现的动力学方程符合物理定律。

Abstract: Dynamical symbolic regression methods identify governing differential equations from noisy data, balancing interpretability and predictive accuracy. However, standard methods often produce expressions that violate known physical laws. To address this, we propose FluxDisco, a physics-informed framework tailored for flux-based, stoichiometric ODE systems. By leveraging a known stoichiometry, we reduce the expression search space and ensure physical adherence. Our framework adapts the Monte Carlo Graph Search algorithm for the unique challenges associated with joint flux discovery of stoichiometric systems. We evaluate our method across a range of physical and biological systems, demonstrating its ability to accurately recover governing dynamics through interpretable equations.

</details>


### [157] [PAC-Bayesian Reconstruction Guarantees for Time Series Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05212)
*Chloé Hashimoto-Cullen,Ghislain Agoua,Benjamin Guedj,Sylvain Le Corff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对时间序列数据的潜在变量模型的 PAC-Bayesian 框架，解决了现有理论分析主要局限于 i.i.d. 设置的问题，并提供了不随轨迹长度增长的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测中广泛使用的生成潜在变量模型（如变分自编码器）缺乏针对非 i.i.d. 时序数据的理论泛化保证，现有理论分析主要局限于独立同分布设置，难以捕捉时间依赖性。

Method: 基于重建误差界，构建适用于马尔可夫潜在结构的 PAC-Bayesian 框架，通过序列生成过程捕捉时间依赖性，并验证了理论假设在实际框架中的可行性。

Result: 推导出了不随轨迹长度增长的泛化误差界，证明了在常见文献假设下该框架的有效性，并提供了具体的示例框架验证假设的可满足性。

Conclusion: 该研究为时间序列潜在变量模型提供了首个 PAC-Bayesian 理论保证，扩展了现有理论至马尔可夫结构，为复杂时序数据的建模提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Forecasting time series accurately is critical for applications with complex data ranging from energy systems to healthcare and finance. Among current state of the art models, generative latent variable models are increasingly implemented; yet principled generalisation guarantees for modern latent variable models remain limited. In particular, while Variational AutoEncoders are widely used for sequential data, their theoretical analysis is largely restricted to i.i.d. settings. In this work, we develop a PAC-Bayesian framework for latent variables models applied to time series. Building on reconstruction-based bounds, we extend PAC-Bayesian guarantees to Markovian latent structures, capturing temporal dependencies through a sequential generative process. These guarantees do not grow with the length of the trajectory. Our bounds depend on assumptions which are common in the literature; we provide an example framework where they would be verified to show that they are not as restrictive as they may seem.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EXAONE Finance，一个专为金融预测设计的无注意力机制时间序列基础模型，通过线性时间算子和掩码上下文增强技术，解决了现有模型在处理长序列、多变量及缺失数据时的计算瓶颈和领域适配问题，并在FinVerse基准测试中取得了全面领先的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）主要针对通用领域设计，依赖计算成本随序列长度和变量数二次增长自注意力机制，且假设输入完全观测，预训练数据未能捕捉金融市场独特动态，难以适应金融领域常见的长序列、多通道及间歇性观测数据面板。

Method: 采用无注意力架构，用两个线性时间算子替代自注意力：1) 用于时间混合的因果一维卷积；2) 用于变量混合的组感知池化多层感知机（MLP）。此外，引入掩码上下文增强策略，在训练期间暴露连续缺失跨度，以提升对金融数据普遍缺失性的鲁棒性。模型在涵盖股票、外汇、商品、加密资产、固定收益及宏观经济指标的大规模金融语料库上进行预训练。

Result: 在涵盖多种资产类别的金融预测基准FinVerse上，EXAONE Finance实现了最先进（SOTA）的性能，在点预测精度、横截面资产排名和投资组合盈利能力三个评估层级中均排名第一。

Conclusion: EXAONE Finance通过架构创新和针对性预训练，有效克服了通用TSFM在金融场景下的局限性，证明了专为金融动态设计的无注意力基础模型在处理复杂金融时间序列任务上的优越性。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [159] [Solving Hard XAI Queries Based on a Compiled Dual-Rail Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04931)
*Arthur Ledaguenel,Florent Capelli,Jean-Marie Lagniez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究布尔分类器的可解释AI(XAI)问题，证明即使在有序二元决策图(OBDD)等可计算表示下，某些溯因解释仍难以计算，但通过双轨编码表示可高效计算这些解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在现实应用中的广泛使用引发了对其可信度的担忧，特别是在关键应用中。XAI领域旨在为用户提供AI系统决策的解释，但计算布尔分类器的解释通常是一个难题。

Method: 通过分析知识编译地图中的有序二元决策图(OBDD)，证明某些溯因解释类(如更短的解释或包含用户偏好的解释)即使在OBDD下仍难以计算，然后提出使用分类器的双轨编码表示来解决这一问题。

Result: 证明了即使在OBDD等可计算表示下，某些溯因解释类仍难以计算；但通过使用分类器的双轨编码的适当表示，可以高效计算这些解释类。

Conclusion: 双轨编码表示能够恢复使用编译表示的优势，使得原本难以计算的溯因解释类(如更短解释和包含用户偏好的解释)可以高效计算，为XAI提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI) within real-world applications has raised a lot of concerns regarding their trustworthiness, especially in critical applications. The field of eXplainable AI (XAI) has emerged with the objective of providing explanations to the users about the decisions made by AI systems. Several explanations for boolean classifiers have been introduced in the literature, including abductive and contrastive explanations, each giving a different insight on the decision of the classifier. However, computing an explanation for a decision of a boolean classifier is a hard problem in general. One way to deal with this complexity is to rely on a compiled representation of the classifier for which each explanation can be computed efficiently. Unfortunately, we prove in this paper that several classes of abductive explanations, remain hard to compute even for Ordered Binary Decision Diagrams, one of the most tractable subsets of the knowledge compilation map. Included in such classes are shorter abductive explanations or abductive explanations that include the explainee's preferences. To recover the benefits of working with compiled representations, we show that a proper representation of the dual-rail encoding of the classifier can be used to compute efficiently these classes of explanations.

</details>


### [160] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI招聘从简单的简历匹配向多阶段工作流（包含证据检索、候选人比较及决策支持）的演变，指出了当前研究在行为标签混淆、数据局限性及隐私评估缺失等方面的差距，并提出了构建可审计、基于证据且兼顾公平与效用的新型评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI招聘系统正经历从自动化“简历对”和“排名列表”向自动化“多阶段工作流”的范式转变，现有的评估方法难以捕捉这种复杂性，且存在行为标签混淆、数据外部效度低、隐私未被直接评估等关键缺口，亟需系统化的梳理与新的评估标准。

Method: 采用系统化叙事综述（systematized narrative review）方法，通过目的性搜索和编码协议（更新至2026年9月2日），整理了40篇代表性作品及相关的工业与法律来源，分析了从双边检索到基于大语言模型（LLM）及工具使用代理的三个耦合转变。

Result: 识别出三个核心转变：从相似性到互惠适用性、从单一模型到复合工作流、从离线预测到与证据及生产力对齐的评估；发现主要缺陷包括行为标签混淆暴露度与资格、私有/合成数据限制外部效度、最终评分掩盖流程失败，且现有研究未联合评估效用、公平性、隐私和安全性。

Conclusion: 提出了从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分级映射框架，并制定了未来议程：构建互惠、基于证据、时间可控、选择性且可审计的系统；进步的标准应在于工作流能否检索正确证据、保留不确定性、支持可争辩的决策，并在明确的成本与风险约束下改善结果。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>


### [161] [Harbor Adapters and Harbor-Index: Infrastructure and a Curated Meta-Dataset for Large-Scale Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04298)
*Lin Shi,Haowei Lin,Zixuan Zhu,Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiang Li,Xiangning Lin,Yaxuan Deng,Han Xu,Yuangang Li,Shanda Li,Zizhao Chen,Hanwen Xing,Harsh Raj,Bo Chen,Quan Shi,Steven Dillmann,Yipeng Gao,Puneesh Khanna,Ruofan Lu,Chao Beyond Zhou,Michael Yang,Robert Zhang,Siyuan Chai,Jiayu Chang,Yizhao Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Yiwei Dai,Wenting Yang,Hange Liu,Minghao Liu,Zihan Wang,Adnan El Assadi,Benedikt Stroebl,E. Kelly Buchanan,Han Meng,Junwei He,Longxuan Yu,Radin Shayanfar,Yukyung Lee,Zhikang Dong,Allen G Hart,Anjiang Wei,Anurag Kashyap,Arpandeep Khatua,Audrey Jixin Zheng,Chengrui Ma,David Heineman,Dubing Chen,Hai-Anh Trinh,Haishuo Fang,Hefan Zhang,Hui Shen,Issa Sugiura,Jiankai Sun,Jiechao Gao,Junhong Lin,Junnan Li,Kai Yang,Lei Hsiung,Maoyu Wang,Mengze Tang,Nabil Omi,Negin Raoof,Nicholas Edwards,Octavia Guo,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Pengliang Ji,Przemysław Hejman,Qi Qi,Qunshu Lin,Richard Zhuang,Rui Yang,Ruichen Zheng,Ryan Marten,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Shizheng Hou,Sicong Jiang,Sijie Li,Song Bian,Terry Yue Zhuo,Tianqing Wu,Tom Tang,Wanjia Zhao,Weihao Xuan,Wenhua Liang,Xian Liu,Xin Lan,Xuan Zhang,Xuandong Zhao,Yanchuan Tang,Yifan Jiang,Yijiang Li,Yitong Guan,Yizhi Li,Yonghui Liu,Yuheng Tang,Yujun,Mao,Yunfei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Zhenheng Tang,Zhifei Li,Ziruo Wang,Ziyu She,Kaiyuan Liu,Iheb Chaabane,Yuxin Tang,Xiangyi Li,Andy Konwinski,Boxuan Li,Leon Liangyu Chen,Alex Dimakis,Nicholas Carlini,Soroush Vosoughi,Di He,Etash Guha,Benjamin Feuer,Mike Merrill,Ludwig Schmidt,Alex Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Harbor Adapters 统一评估基础设施，将80+基准适配至任意智能体，并通过大规模评估（8模型×54基准）揭示能力与失败模式，最终构建 Harbor-Index（82高难度任务）实现高效可靠的智能体评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准评估面临环境复杂、集成困难的问题，缺乏统一且可扩展的评估基础设施，导致评估成本高、覆盖范围有限。

Method: 1. 开发基准适配器，将80+基准移植以评估任意智能体，并通过代码审查和一致性实验验证；2. 对8个模型在54个基准上进行大规模评估，使用Terminus-2及3种原生框架；3. 构建 Harbor-Index，通过难度过滤、AI与人工审计及修复循环筛选82个高质量任务。

Result: 1. 成功适配80+基准并验证其有效性；2. 大规模评估揭示了不同模型的能力差异与失败模式；3. Harbor-Index 中所有模型-框架配置通过率均低于30%，最强模型（GPT-5.5 with Codex）仅达28.0%，证明其高难度与区分度。

Conclusion: Harbor Adapters 与 Harbor-Index 为语言模型智能体提供了可靠、全面且低成本的评估方案，相关工具与数据已开源，推动智能体评估标准化。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>


### [162] [Data-Optimized Contingency Screening: A Machine Learning Approach to Power System Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04300)
*Joshua Salako,Folajimi Osikomaiya,Olakorede Olamiju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习算法（RF、SVM、KNN）结合SMOTE和PCA预处理技术，对电力系统N-k故障场景进行安全等级分类（安全、中等、严重），在IEEE-14和IEEE-30总线系统上验证了随机森林（RF）具有最佳分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统的安全性对稳定性和可靠性至关重要，尤其是在发生中断时。有效的故障分类能够实现主动决策，从而减轻大规模停电和故障风险。传统方法在实时评估和扩展性上存在局限，需探索更高效的替代方案。

Method: 采用牛顿 - 拉夫逊潮流法提取故障场景数据，以整体性能指数（OPI）作为安全度量。利用SMOTE解决类别不平衡问题，PCA降低数据维度。构建了四种混合预处理配置（归一化、SMOTE平衡、PCA转换、SMOTE+PCA组合），并在IEEE-14和IEEE-30总线系统的N-k（k=1,2,3）故障数据集上训练和评估KNN、随机森林（RF）和SVM模型。

Result: 随机森林（RF）表现最佳，在IEEE-30和IEEE-14系统中分别达到0.97和0.86的F1分数。SVM从PCA中显著受益并提高了分类准确率，KNN最适合SMOTE和PCA转换。研究发现PCA对整体性能的贡献大于SMOTE；SMOTE虽提高了召回率，但可能引入假阳性，需在准确率上做出权衡。

Conclusion: 机器学习是一种可扩展且强大的传统故障分析替代方案，能够显著提升电力系统安全评估的实时性。其中，随机森林结合适当的预处理策略在严重故障分类中表现最优。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [163] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Iris-mini和Iris-pro两个搜索代理，提出了从网页语料库反向构建任务的数据流水线，并采用"SFT-RL爬升”策略进行训练，在多个基准测试中取得了开源搜索代理的最强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有搜索代理在复杂多跳推理任务中的局限性，通过构建无法通过字符串匹配解决、必须依赖证据支持的高难度任务，并优化推理时的上下文管理，以提升模型在真实搜索场景中的表现。

Method: 1. 数据构建：基于网页语料库的超链接结构反向构建多跳任务链，将非答案实体重写为描述性引用以消除字符串匹配线索，仅保留闭卷失败但提供证据后可解的问题。
2. 训练策略：采用"SFT-RL爬升”循环，先进行监督微调（SFT），再利用实时搜索进行强化学习（RL）优化策略；RL过程中动态中断并恢复过长 rollout，并将每轮RL中最难解决且最高效的轨迹反馈至下一轮SFT。
3. 评估设置：固定工具集、上下文限制和评判标准，对比开启与关闭推理时上下文管理的效果，且仅使用单一ReAct代理，无子代理或测试时验证。

Result: 在开启上下文管理的情况下，Iris-mini和Iris-pro在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE基准上分别达到了82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3和88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4的得分，是其参数量级中表现最强的开源搜索代理。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的任务构建流水线和"SFT-RL爬升”训练范式，结合高效的推理时上下文管理，显著提升了搜索代理的性能；作者计划开源模型权重及完整的数据构建、训练和评估方案。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [164] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需修改模型参数的测试时方法，通过移除解释中未提及的输入概念并重新查询模型，有效解决了大语言模型解释中的“不完整性”问题，显著提升了解释的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在关键决策中的应用日益增多，但其生成的解释往往缺乏忠实度（unfaithful），即不能真实反映模型的推理过程。现有的提升忠实度的方法要么需要访问模型权重且计算成本高（训练时方法），要么主要侧重于解决“不合理性”（unsoundness，即引用了未影响结果的因素），而忽视了“不完整性”（incompleteness，即遗漏了影响结果的因素）。

Method: 作者提出了一种模型无关的测试时方法：首先获取模型对原始输入的回答和解释，然后从输入中移除解释中未 credited（未归因）的概念，构建缩减后的输入并重新查询模型。该方法旨在消除未提及因素的影响，同时保留已提及概念的影响，从而直接针对解释的不完整性进行优化。

Result: 在两个数据集、多个模型家族以及两种独立的忠实度指标上的实验表明，该方法相比标准提示（standard prompting）和鼓励忠实度的提示（prompting to encourage faithfulness），显著提高了解释的忠实度。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种灵活、高效的机制，可在推理阶段无需修改模型参数的情况下减少隐藏影响，从而提升大语言模型辅助决策的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [165] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提升大语言模型（LLM）的个体能力可能导致系统级结果恶化，因为共享架构和训练会导致高能力模型行为高度相关，从而产生不可分散的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 正被大规模部署于金融、内容审核等关键系统中，但现有研究多关注个体模型能力的提升，忽视了个体能力增强可能对系统级稳定性产生的负面影响，特别是由行为同质化引发的系统性风险。

Method: 构建了一个通用理论框架来阐述行为相关性如何产生不可分散的风险底线，并通过基于智能体的模拟实验，在金融市场中测试了不同通用能力的 LLM 交易者的行为及其对市场风险的影响。

Result: 1. 前沿 LLM 表现出显著的行为相关性，且随能力提升而增强；2. 当共享推理准确时，增加代理参与度可降低市场风险；3. 当代理处于共同的错误信息环境中时，这种相关性行为会转化为系统性负债。

Conclusion: 存在“能力悖论”：单纯提升个体模型能力并不必然带来更好的系统级结果，共享训练导致的同质化行为在特定环境下（如错误信息）会加剧系统风险，其他领域是否存在类似动态仍需实证研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [166] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出企业语言模型（CLM）框架，旨在解决企业AI部署中因缺乏结构化决策与执行基础而导致的失败问题。CLM通过神经符号网格、技能图谱、活体数字孪生和深度安全层四大支柱，将企业的显性与隐性知识转化为本体论基础，实现可解释的推理与受控执行，并在巴西一家医院进行了初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业AI部署失败并非源于模型能力不足，而是组织缺乏编码其决策、协商与执行过程的结构化基础。通用大语言模型（LLM）缺乏企业特定的本体论先验，检索增强生成（RAG）脆弱且无法转化为可执行动作，静态操作手册虽编码逻辑但缺乏推理与自适应能力。因此，需要一种从设计之初就协同考虑隐性知识捕获、本体论基础、主权部署及可审计执行的架构。

Method: 提出企业语言模型（CLM）框架，包含五个能力平面和四大架构支柱：1）神经符号网格（Neurosymbolic Mesh），耦合生成模型与知识图谱；2）技能图谱（Skill Graph），对可复用的战术、角色、异议和目标进行类型化与组合；3）活体数字孪生（Living Digital Twins），将功能领域建模为推理代理；4）深度安全层（Deep Security Layer），强制执行主权、可追溯性及人类监督。此外，采用“规范即代码”（Spec-as-Code）范式连接基础意图与可执行产物。

Result: 定义了CLM作为一个独立的研究对象；引入了技能图谱以实现构建级的组合可解释性；提出了“智慧监听者”（Wisdom Listener）效应，即具备隐性知识能力的基础模型随使用而价值复合增长，关联动态能力与组织学习；并通过巴西一家JCI认证的三级医院案例，在LGPD法规下实例化了六个成熟度阶段中的三个阶段，提供了实证证据。

Conclusion: CLM代表了一种以基础为中心（foundation-centric）的新型企业AI架构类别，通过将隐性知识本体化、推理过程结构化及执行动作受控化，有效填补了通用AI模型与企业具体运营逻辑之间的鸿沟，为构建具备自适应能力和合规性的企业智能系统提供了理论框架与实践路径。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed. CLM has five capability planes and four architectural pillars: a Neurosymbolic Mesh coupling generative models with a knowledge graph; a Skill Graph where reusable tactics, personas, objections, and goals are typed and composed; Living Digital Twins modeling functional areas as reasoning surrogates; and a Deep Security Layer enforcing sovereignty, traceability, and human oversight. A Spec-as-Code paradigm bridges grounded intent and executable artifact. CLM is one instantiation of this foundation-centric class. Four contributions follow: CLM is defined as a distinct object of study; the Skill Graph is introduced for compositional explainability by construction; the Wisdom Listener effect is proposed, whereby tacit-capable foundations compound in value with use, connecting to dynamic capabilities and organizational learning; and evidence from a JCI-accredited tertiary hospital in Brazil instantiates three of the six maturity stages under LGPD.

</details>


### [167] [HarvestBench: Measuring Whether LLM Agents Will Pay to Avoid Killing Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04444)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Miles Tidmarsh,Matthias Endres,Anshuman Singh,Jeremiah Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarvestBench 是首个通过模拟农场环境，量化大语言模型（LLM）为避免伤害生物所愿支付“代价”的基准测试。研究发现，不同模型对动物的伤害率差异巨大（0.4%-98.8%），且与模型能力无关；价格机制和道德简报显著影响模型决策，该基准通过游戏日志计数而非LLM评分，确保了结果的可复现性与行为测量的客观性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的副作用基准测试缺乏对避免副作用的“定价”机制，且未明确将副作用对象定义为生物。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过模拟环境测量模型在实际决策中为避免伤害生物愿意付出的具体代价，而非仅依赖模型的语言陈述。

Method: 构建名为 HarvestBench 的强化学习网格世界农场模拟环境。LLM 子代理控制两辆拖拉机进行合作收割，环境中包含动物、岩石和干草捆。当动物阻挡路线时，模型需在“直接碾压（无燃料成本）”和“绕行（需支付标价的燃料成本）”之间做出无记忆决策。实验对比了模型对动物、岩石（损坏拖拉机）和干草捆（无害）的处理差异，并测试了价格敏感性及道德简报的影响。评分基于游戏日志的事件计数，不依赖 LLM 评估器。

Result: 在9个模型的7,201次定价决策中，动物相关决策占3,951次。模型间的动物致死率差异极大（0.4%至98.8%），且未随模型能力排序（Terra 和 Sol 最仁慈，GPT-4o-mini 最残酷）。6个模型中有4个对价格表现出显著敏感性（弹性系数0.09-1.69）。所有模型在默认及各类地图几何结构下，碾压野生动物的频率均高于家养动物。道德简报效果显著：包含简报时，6个推理模型中有5个的致死率低于6%；移除简报后，所有6个模型的致死率升至84%以上。

Conclusion: HarvestBench 成功建立了一种可复现的、基于行为而非言语的评估框架，能够量化模型为避免伤害生物所愿支付的成本。研究揭示了模型在道德决策上的巨大差异及其对价格信号和上下文提示（如道德简报）的高度敏感性，证明了通过机制设计引导模型道德行为的有效性。

Abstract: Benchmarks for the side effects an agent causes on the way to a goal already exist, but HarvestBench is the first to put a price on avoiding the side effect and to name that side effect as a living creature. It is a farm simulation: LLM sub-agents drive a crew of two tractors through a cooperative corn harvest, with animals in the field. The environment is a reinforcement learning gridworld, every decision is made without memory, and the harm is never named in the goal. When an animal blocks a tractor's route the autopilot stops and asks the model whether to drive on, at no fuel cost, or swerve around it for a posted fuel price. Kills are compared against two controls: rocks, which damage the tractor and are hit under 1% of the time by every model, and hay bales, which are harmless and not alive. Models can also take crops from the neighbor's field instead of their own, a second test of what they treat as moral.
  Across nine models and 7,201 priced decisions, 3,951 involved an animal rather than a hay bale or a rock. Kill rates range from 0.4% to 98.8%, with Terra and Sol the most merciful and GPT-4o-mini the most cruel, and they are not ordered by capability. Four of six models were sensitive to price at the 5% level, with elasticities from 0.09 to 1.69. All nine drove over wild animals more often than farmed animals on the default map, and the direction held at every map geometry in every model with room to move. The briefing mattered most: under the morality briefing the kill rate was under 6% in five of six reasoning models, and removing it raised the kill rate above 84% in all six.
  HarvestBench uses no LLM grader. The scorer counts events in the game log, so it is fully reproducible, and it measures what a model will pay to avoid harm rather than what it says about harm.

</details>


### [168] [PerfReasoning: How Well Do LLMs Reason on Hardware Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04476)
*Dan Zhao,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam,Christos Kozyrakis,Qijing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PerfReasoning基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在硬件性能建模方面的能力。结果显示，现有模型在直接推理任务上表现良好（闭源模型>90%），但在生成可执行的性能模型代码方面极具挑战性（除GPT-5.6 Sol外，其他模型通过率均低于15%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能建模是硬件设计和软件优化的核心，但构建这些模型需要对计算、数据复用、存储和移动进行结构化推理。目前缺乏专门评估LLM在此领域能力的基准，难以区分模型“看似合理”的推理与“可靠”的模型构建能力之间的差距。

Method: 引入PerfReasoning基准，设定工作负载、架构和映射规范，评估LLM两种角色：1) 作为直接性能推理者（回答比较映射、预测片外流量和缓冲区需求等问题）；2) 作为分析性性能模型代码的生成器。此外，还测试了特定任务强化学习（RL）和无反馈多轮自修正提示的效果。

Result: 最强的闭源模型在基于推理的问答中准确率超过90%，最佳开源权重模型达到82.4%。然而，模型构建任务难度显著：仅GPT-5.6 Sol通过率超过80%，其他配置平均低于15%且运行间差异大。特定任务RL使4B模型的映射推理准确率提升15.7个百分点，而无反馈的多轮自修正提示效果不可靠。

Conclusion: PerfReasoning揭示了LLM在架构推理的似是而非性与构建可靠性能模型能力之间的巨大差距。该基准将公开释放，以支持可复现的评估并追踪未来的进展。

Abstract: Performance modeling is central to hardware design and software optimization, yet constructing these models requires structured reasoning about computation, data reuse, storage, and movement. We introduce PerfReasoning, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs both as direct performance reasoners and as generators of analytical performance-model code. Given workload, architecture, and mapping specifications, models compare mappings and predict off-chip traffic and buffer requirements. The strongest closed-source models exceed 90% on reasoning-based Q&A, and the best open-weight model reaches 82.4%. However, model construction is substantially harder: while GPT-5.6 Sol exceeds 80% pass rate, all other model configurations average below 15% and vary markedly across runs. Task-specific RL raises a 4B model's mapping-reasoning accuracy by 15.7 points, whereas feedback-free multi-round self-revision prompting is not reliably effective. PerfReasoning exposes the gap between plausible architectural reasoning and reliable performance-model construction. We will publicly release the benchmark to support reproducible evaluation and track future progress.

</details>


### [169] [When Quantization Breaks Memory: Recurrent-State Write-Back in Low-Precision Temporal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04490)
*Ismail Erbas,Xavier Intes,Vikas Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了循环神经网络（RNN）量化中“循环状态写回”（recurrent-state write-back）规则对推理精度的关键影响，发现低精度状态存储会导致误差剧增，并提出了无需重新训练的误差反馈等机制来恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化虽能降低神经网络推理的计算和内存需求，但在循环网络中，量化后的状态会被存储并在下一时间步返回，这种状态存储规则（即写回机制）会改变后续计算，导致传统量化方法在RNN中失效，其具体影响机制尚不明确。

Method: 在用于荧光寿命成像的紧凑GRU编码器 - 解码器模型中，固定训练好的模型，将连续状态传播替换为确定性4位状态存储以隔离写回效应；引入误差反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆机制来补偿被抑制的更新；并在独立训练的LSTM模型中重复实验以验证通用性。

Result: 将连续状态替换为4位存储导致短寿命分量τ1和长寿命分量τ2的估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍；失败原因是微小更新低于写入阈值导致状态停滞；引入误差反馈等机制可在不重新训练的情况下恢复精度；精度扫描显示增加状态精度有时反而恶化结果，而匹配训练可学习适应状态接口；LSTM实验证实了该现象的普遍性及细胞状态对量化更敏感。

Conclusion: 循环状态写回是决定低精度循环动态的关键因素，状态存储接口是量化循环推理的核心设计考量，通过特定的状态接口干预（如误差反馈）可有效解决低精度下的性能崩溃问题。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the computational and memory demands of neural-network inference. In recurrent networks, however, the quantized state is stored and returned at the next time step, so the rule used to store that state can alter subsequent computations. Here, we introduce recurrent-state write-back to denote this rule and isolate its effect in a compact GRU encoder--decoder for fluorescence lifetime imaging, a molecular imaging modality used in quantitative biological imaging. A central task is estimating two lifetime parameters, the short-lived component τ1 and the long-lived component τ2, from high-noise time-resolved fluorescence signals. Holding the trained model fixed, replacing continuous state propagation with deterministic 4-bit state storage increases estimation errors for τ1 and τ2 by approximately 70x and 300x, respectively. Failure occurs when repeated small updates remain below the write threshold, leaving the stored state nearly fixed while the network continues to propose change. Error feedback, residual memory, and direction memory carry information from these suppressed updates across time and recover accuracy without retraining. Precision sweeps show that increasing state precision can worsen a fixed recurrent solution, while matched training shows that compatibility with the state interface can be learned. To test whether this behavior extends beyond the GRU, we repeat the post-training intervention in an independently trained LSTM, where coarse write-back reproduces the failure, error feedback restores accuracy, and state-specific interventions reveal greater sensitivity of the cell state than the hidden state. Our results establish recurrent-state write-back as a key determinant of low-precision recurrent dynamics and identify the state-storage interface as a central design consideration for quantized recurrent inference.

</details>


### [170] [ResLearn-XR: Residual Learning for Network Traffic and Quality-of-Experience-Aware Modeling in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04493)
*Yoga Suhas Kuruba Manjunath,Jie Gao,Lian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ResLearn-XR，一个用于预测扩展现实（XR）网络流量和评估体验质量（QoE）风险的两阶段残差学习框架，通过引入数据描述算法（DDA）和新构建的数据集，显著提升了预测精度和风险估计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决XR流量具有突发性、非平稳性的动态特征，以及在加密流量环境下难以准确进行连续流量预测和 probabilistic QoE 风险评估的挑战。

Method: 采用两阶段时序学习结构：基础序列预测模型结合特定任务的残差学习组件。在值空间进行连续流量预测，在logit空间进行概率QoE风险估计。引入因果特征构建模块——数据描述算法（DDA），将包级应用层可观测数据转换为感知帧时序的描述符，并构建了配对连续流量迹线与用户报告QoE标签的XR Traffic-QoE数据集。

Result: 在帧数、帧大小和到达间隔时间的预测上，SMAPE降低了高达17.84%；在QoE风险估计上，相较于单阶段基线模型，SMAPE降低了高达87.8%。

Conclusion: ResLearn-XR框架通过残差学习机制和专门的特征工程，有效适应了XR流量的复杂动态，实现了高精度的流量预测和极低的QoE风险估计误差，为优化XR网络服务质量提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: We present ResLearn-XR, a residual learning framework for predicting eXtended Reality (XR) network traffic and estimating Quality-of-Experience (QoE) risk. ResLearn-XR adopts a two-stage temporal learning structure comprising a base sequence prediction model augmented with task-specific residual learning components to improve adaptability to bursty, non-stationary XR traffic dynamics. The residual learning stages operate in the value space for continuous XR traffic forecasting and in the logit space for probabilistic QoE risk estimation. \rev{For the QoE-risk branch, we introduce a Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA), a causal feature-construction module that converts packet-level application-layer observables into frame-timing-aware descriptors suitable for encrypted traffic analysis. We also construct an XR Traffic-QoE dataset that pairs continuous XR traffic traces with session-level user-reported QoE labels. ResLearn-XR reduces SMAPE by up to 17.84% across frame-count, frame-size, and inter-arrival-time prediction, while reducing QoE-risk estimation SMAPE by up to 87.8% over single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [171] [Rethinking Indirect Prompt Injection as a Test-Time Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04495)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Joon Sik Kim,Blazej Manczak,Vaikkunth Mugunthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将间接提示注入建模为测试时的搜索问题，并提出了一种具备环境侦察、策略推理和自适应评估能力的智能体攻击者。研究发现，增加攻击者的测试时计算量可提升漏洞发现率，且明确的策略管理对避免冗余搜索至关重要，表明安全评估应同时考量攻击者的搜索过程与计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全评估往往将攻击成功视为与预算无关的属性，忽视了攻击者针对系统攻击面进行自适应搜索的过程。本文旨在揭示这种自适应搜索是使用工具的智能体（tool-using agents）中一个重要但未被充分探索的安全风险。

Method: 提出了一种带有专用搜索框架的智能体攻击者（agentic attacker）。该方法将间接提示注入形式化为测试时搜索，具体包含三个核心步骤：环境侦察（environment reconnaissance）、对攻击策略的结构化推理（structured reasoning），以及利用受害者智能体反馈进行的自适应评估（adaptive evaluation）。

Result: 实验表明，在异构任务中，增加攻击者的测试时计算量（test-time compute）能显著提高漏洞的发现和利用效率。消融实验进一步证明，显式的策略管理对于避免冗余搜索并在较大计算预算下维持收益增益至关重要。

Conclusion: 智能体安全评估不应仅关注攻击结果，而应同时刻画攻击者的搜索过程及其计算预算。攻击者对系统攻击面的自适应搜索构成了使用工具的智能体的关键安全风险，需要在未来的研究中得到更多关注。

Abstract: We formulate indirect prompt injection as a test-time search over a task-dependent attack surface induced by the environment, user task, and injection task. To operationalize this formulation, we introduce an agentic attacker with a dedicated search harness that performs environment reconnaissance, structured reasoning over attack strategies, and adaptive evaluation using victim-agent feedback. Across heterogeneous tasks, we find that increasing attacker test-time compute improves vulnerability discovery and exploitation, while ablations show that explicit strategy management is important for avoiding redundant search and sustaining gains at larger budgets. These results suggest that agentic security evaluations should characterize both the attacker's search procedure and compute budget, rather than treating attack success as a budget-independent property of the victim. More broadly, our findings identify the attacker's adaptive search over the system attack surfaces as an important and underexplored security risk for tool-using agents.

</details>


### [172] [BioSync: Transformer-Based Cross-Modal Fusion for a Multimodal Physiological Digital Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04504)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioSync 提出了一种基于多头自注意力和线性分支的深度学习架构，用于融合可穿戴设备的心率、脑电、行为和语音等多模态数据，生成连续的“BioSync 指数 (BSI)”数字生物标志物。在合成队列评估中，该模型在认知衰退和代谢自主神经任务上的表现优于传统的特征拼接方法，且对数据噪声和模态缺失具有更强的鲁棒性，能有效反映潜在的生理严重程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可穿戴和移动设备测量数据（心脏、神经、行为、语音）仅提供部分且对噪声敏感的生理状态视图。研究旨在解决如何有效融合这些多模态数据以提取单模态无法获取的联合信息，并基于潜变量测量理论，构建一个能准确反映潜在生理严重程度的连续复合数字生物标志物。

Method: 提出了 BioSync 模型，该模型在 BEST 框架下定义 BioSync 指数 (BSI)。架构上采用多头自注意力机制处理模态 token，并增加一个线性分支，其假设类包含标准的特征拼接（即宽深结合架构）。模型在两个文献支持的合成队列上进行评估：一个是包含 HRV、EEG、活动记录和语音的四模态认知衰退队列，另一个是基于 AI-READI 可穿戴模式的代谢 - 自主神经队列。此外，还进行了纯注意力消融实验和模态丢弃（modality-dropout）鲁棒性测试。

Result: 在认知队列中，BioSync 的 AUC 为 0.928，略高于拼接法的 0.926；在代谢队列中，BioSync 的准确率/F1 值为 0.764/0.766，优于拼接法的 0.756/0.758。BSI 与两个队列中的潜在严重程度均高度相关（r 分别为 0.91 和 0.68）。消融实验表明，结合宽深架构带来了性能提升（纯注意力 AUC 为 0.911）。在模态缺失的鲁棒性测试中，BioSync 在多数腐蚀率下表现优于拼接法，且其认知队列 AUC 高于五个已发布的数字生物标志物参考值。

Conclusion: BioSync 模型通过结合自注意力机制和线性分支，成功整合了多模态生理数据，生成的 BSI 指标在准确性、鲁棒性及与潜在严重程度的相关性上均优于传统特征拼接方法。该研究明确了模型的计算特性，但指出仍需在真实队列中进行进一步验证以确立其临床效用。

Abstract: Cardiac, neural, behavioral, and speech measurements from wearable and mobile devices provide partial, noise-sensitive views of physiological state. BioSync combines these measurements into the \textbf{BioSync Index (BSI)}, a continuous composite digital biomarker defined under the BEST framework. The model applies multi-head self-attention to modality tokens and adds a linear branch whose hypothesis class includes standard feature concatenation. This architecture is motivated by latent-variable measurement theory and by the possibility that joint observations contain information unavailable from individual modalities. We evaluated BioSync on two literature-informed synthetic cohorts: a four-modality cognitive-decline cohort using HRV, EEG, actigraphy, and speech, and a metabolic-autonomic cohort structured around the public AI-READI wearable schema. In the cognitive cohort, BioSync and concatenation obtained AUCs of 0.928 and 0.926, respectively. In the metabolic cohort, BioSync obtained accuracy/F1 of 0.764/0.766, compared with 0.756/0.758 for concatenation. The BSI correlated with latent severity in both cohorts ($r=0.91$ and $r=0.68$). A pure-attention ablation obtained cognitive-cohort AUC 0.911, locating the increase to 0.928 in the combined wide-and-deep architecture. With matched modality-dropout training, BioSync led concatenation at five of six cognitive-cohort corruption rates and at the highest metabolic-cohort rate. Its cognitive-cohort AUC was also higher than five published digital-biomarker reference values, although differences in datasets and tasks preclude a controlled benchmark claim. Comparison with single-modality, early-fusion, and late-fusion designs across six prespecified criteria identifies the model's computational properties; validation on real cohorts remains necessary.

</details>


### [173] [What Does Multi-Harness RL Learn? Credit Assignment and Portability in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04518)
*Chenqian Le,Jiayi Cheng,Qijia He,Runhao Li,Yinghao Li,Xupeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在仓库级代码生成的强化学习中，评估工具（harness）的选择对模型性能影响巨大（提升4.3倍），远超训练策略（组内对比vs跨组对比）的影响；跨工具信用分配并未带来比组内信用分配更通用的能力，且其优势主要源于对特定工具配置的适应而非真正的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在多工具（multi-harness）强化学习食谱中，将策略暴露于多个工具与在相对优势组内比较奖励这两种选择的具体贡献，特别是隔离并评估“组相对策略优化（GRPO）”中分组规则（组内Within vs 跨组Cross）对代码生成代理泛化能力的实际影响。

Method: 基于Qwen3-8B监督热启动，重放来自Aider、OpenHands等四个来源的冻结任务 - 工具记录；在相同更新次数下，对比两种GRPO规则（Within：每个任务 - 工具对为一组；Cross：任务内工具池化）；使用密封的SWE-bench Verified预言机在四个源工具和一个未见于训练的持出工具上进行评估；通过重收集在线数据和分类器分析进一步验证结果。

Result: 评估工具是主导变量，使平均解决率从2.14%提升至9.27%（4.3倍），而训练规则仅带来1.16倍变化；Cross与Within规则在持出工具上的性能差异极小（+0.25个百分点，置信区间包含0），且小于各自种子间的波动；分类器显示Cross的优势可被用来识别生成工具（+4.48 pp），表明其优势源于配置适应而非通用能力提升；两种规则在各自工具内的动作分布和得分一致。

Conclusion: 跨工具信用分配仅产生配置适应性，并未提供比组内信用分配更具可迁移性的能力；未来的多工具强化学习报告必须明确声明分组边界，并在未见过的工具下进行测试以验证真正的泛化性。

Abstract: Agent reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly runs through full execution harnesses, and a multi-harness recipe mixes two choices: exposing the policy to several harnesses, and comparing their rewards inside one relative-advantage group. We isolate the second choice in repository-level coding. From one Qwen3-8B supervised warm start we replay the same frozen task-harness records from Aider, OpenHands, Qwen Code, and SWE-agent, with the same number of updates, under two rules for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), Within (one group per task-harness pair) and Cross (harnesses pooled within a task), and score every checkpoint with a sealed SWE-bench Verified oracle on four source harnesses and a minimal harness held out of training. The evaluation harness is the dominant variable: across 24,000 sealed evaluations it moves the mean solve rate from 2.14\% to 9.27\%, a factor of 4.3, where the training recipe moves it by 1.16. The grouping rule is not. On the held-out harness, Cross minus Within is +0.25 pp, 95\% confidence interval [-0.48, +1.02], at eight attempts per task, and +0.16 [-0.41, +0.72] pooled over three training seeds whose individual estimates change sign. Each rule's own seed range, 0.42 to 0.45 pp, exceeds the difference between them. Both rules place their largest gains on the same source harness. The pooled advantage carries the harness: an out-of-fold classifier recovers the generating harness from Cross's advantage +4.48 pp above the shuffled-label baseline and from Within's not at all, and the two rules still reach the same held-out score and action distribution inside each harness. Re-collecting half the training data on-policy does not change this. Cross-harness credit yields configuration adaptation and no more portable capability than within-harness credit. Multi-harness RL reports should state the grouping boundary and test under an unseen harness.

</details>


### [174] [MaxKernel: Agentic Kernel Generation for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04523)
*Shangkun Wang,Nina Cai,Charles Hoong,Julian Walker,Gerson Kroiz,George Vanica,Deepak Patil,Andi Gavrilescu,Hassan Sipra,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MaxKernel，一个用于 TPU 内核开发的多智能体系统，包含人机协作、自主优化和图搜索三种范式，在 JaxBench 基准测试中表现优异，可生成与专家手写代码性能相当的高效内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为加速器设计高性能自定义内核需要深厚的硬件专业知识，任务复杂。利用大语言模型结合实时编译器反馈构建智能体系统，可降低门槛并自动化内核生成过程。

Method: MaxKernel 实现三种开发范式：(1) 人机协作智能体逐步设计；(2) 自主智能体执行指标驱动的自动优化循环；(3) 基于图的自主搜索扩展设计空间探索。系统共享专用子智能体池处理规划、实现、调试、测试和硬件分析。

Result: 在包含 50 个多样化 TPU 内核任务的 JaxBench 及真实工作负载上评估，MaxKernel 生成的实现高度优化，性能匹配专家手写基线，在基准测试中表现显著。

Conclusion: MaxKernel 成功展示了多智能体系统在内核生成中的有效性，能够自动化产生高性能 TPU 内核，系统已开源。

Abstract: Designing and authoring high-performance custom kernels for accelerators is a complex task that requires deep hardware-level expertise. Large Language Models (LLM) can be leveraged together with real-time compiler feedback to build agentic systems for kernel generation. In this work, we present MaxKernel, a multi-agent system that implements three distinct paradigms for TPU kernel development: (1) a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) agent for collaborative, step-by-step design; (2) an Autonomous (Auto) agent that executes a fully automated, metric/trace-driven optimization loop; and (3) a Graph-Based Autonomous Search that scales the Auto agent for global exploration of the design space. All three paradigms leverage a shared pool of specialized sub-agents to handle planning, implementation, self-debugging, testing, and hardware profiling. We evaluate MaxKernel on JaxBench, a comprehensive suite of 50 diverse kernel tasks for TPUs, alongside complex, real-world workloads from state-of-the-art open-source models. We demonstrate that MaxKernel consistently generates highly optimized implementations, matching expert hand-tuned baselines and delivering significant performance across the benchmark. Our agent is open-sourced and available https://github.com/AI-Hypercomputer/accelerator-agents/tree/main/MaxKernel.

</details>


### [175] [Towards a universal language of concepts: A survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04528)
*Aishni Parab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出程序作为概念的通用表示形式，回顾了使用程序进行概念学习的计算模型，并评估其对通用表示语言的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够从稀疏数据中学习和泛化新概念，因为他们以丰富的结构形式表达知识。因此，需要探索一种通用的概念表示形式来模拟这种能力。

Method: 提出程序作为概念的强候选通用表示形式，并回顾现有的使用程序作为概念表示的计算模型。

Result: 评估了这些计算模型在构建通用表示语言方面的贡献。

Conclusion: 程序是表示概念的有力候选者，有助于实现通用表示语言的目标。

Abstract: Humans can learn and generalize novel concepts from sparse data because they express knowledge in rich structural formats. In this paper, we propose that programs are a strong candidate for universal representation of concepts. We review computational models of concept learning that use programs as their concept representation and evaluate their contribution toward a universal representational language.

</details>


### [176] [Data-Driven Discovery of Composition-Dependent Constitutive Models for Hyperelasticity and Viscoelasticity of Digital Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04541)
*Josué García-Ávila,Beijun Shen,Manuel K. Rausch,Mary C. Boyce,Adrián Buganza-Tepole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的多材料本构模型框架，用于描述多材料3D打印数字材料的非线性、成分依赖和速率依赖的粘弹性行为，该框架在保持经典模型结构的同时，利用神经网络自动构建应变能函数和学习动力学参数，实现了跨材料和加载速率的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典有限应变粘弹性模型使用闭式应变能函数和内部变量演化来描述多材料3D打印数字材料的复杂行为，但单一本构模型在跨材料和加载速率泛化时灵活性有限。

Method: 提出一种数据驱动的多材料本构建模框架，推广Bergström和Boyce的公式，保留经典模型结构（乘法运动学、基于不变量的应变能函数、标量耗散演化律），平衡分支通过直接预测闭式模型参数或使用神经常微分方程（NODEs）构建多凸应变能函数，非平衡分支动力学通过识别闭式参数或使用约束人工神经网络学习。

Result: 利用多材料成分的多速率单轴压缩数据验证，该框架能够捕捉跨成分的速率依赖刚度和滞后现象，同时保持热力学一致性。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动框架在保持经典模型物理结构的同时，通过机器学习方法实现了本构模型参数的自动发现和函数构建，有效解决了多材料3D打印数字材料复杂粘弹性行为的建模泛化问题。

Abstract: Digital materials fabricated by multi-material 3D printing are designed as controlled mixtures of stiff and compliant constituents, yielding effective responses that span more than an order of magnitude in apparent stiffness and exhibit strongly nonlinear, composition-dependent, and rate-dependent dissipative behavior. Classical finite-strain viscoelastic models represent such behavior with closed-form strain energy functions for equilibrium and non-equilibrium stresses as well as evolution of internal variables, which may limit flexibility when a single constitutive model is expected to generalize across materials and loading rates. Here, we present a data-driven multi-material constitutive modeling framework that generalizes a formulation by Bergström and Boyce. The proposed framework retains the structure of the classical model, namely multiplicative kinematics, invariant-based strain-energy functions, and a scalar dissipative evolution law directed along the normalized nonequilibrium deviatoric stress. For the equilibrium branch, the data-driven discovery framework either directly predicts closed-form model parameters as functions of composition or automatically constructs a polyconvex strain-energy function using neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs). The nonequilibrium branch kinetics are learned similarly, either by directly identifying closed-form parameters across compositions or by using appropriately constrained artificial neural networks. Using multi-rate uniaxial compression data across multiple material compositions, we show that the proposed formulation captures rate-dependent stiffness and hysteresis across compositions while preserving thermodynamic consistency.

</details>


### [177] [From Answers to Interpretations: Rethinking Ambiguity-Induced Aleatoric Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04543)
*Omer Nahum,Niv Nayman,Jonathan Fhima,Alon Zolfi,Jeremy Levy,Shai Mazor,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于“澄清”（clarification-only）的方法来估计大语言模型中的偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty），证明了无需生成答案即可识别输入歧义，该方法在提升检测精度（AUROC 63.34 vs 60.85）的同时，显著降低了计算成本并减少了与认知不确定性（epistemic uncertainty）的混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决可靠部署大语言模型时的关键挑战：区分任务中不可减少的变异性（偶然不确定性，主要源于输入歧义或描述不清）与模型知识局限（认知不确定性）。现有的分解方法通过生成澄清并查询答案来估计不确定性，但作者认为生成答案往往是冗余的、增加成本，且可能因“认知泄漏”（epistemic leakage）导致误导。

Method: 提出一种“仅澄清”（clarification-only）方法，直接从 plausible interpretations（合理解释空间）中估计由歧义引起的偶然不确定性分量，完全摒弃了对澄清后输入生成答案的步骤。该方法在理论上支持了答案对于识别歧义的非必要性，并在三个基准测试上进行了操作性评估。
