We further develop an affinity-guided collusive bidding strategy that uses mutual reviewer-paper affinities to construct collusion rings and select target papers, producing expertise-consistent rather than arbitrarily targeted attacks. Controlled experiments show that collusive bidding more than doubles target-paper capture and that assigned colluders score target papers about two points higher than honest co-reviewers, while conference-wide effects remain comparatively modest. Evaluated bid-phase detectors provide only limited evidence of collusion: in a fixed-triplet detector stress test, native positive-bid graphs are confounded by benign affinity, while a Very-High-only diagnostic view enables precise but low-coverage local recovery.

</details>


### [245] [Multi-Step Tool-Calling over Korean Open Public APIs: A Benchmark and a Data-Synthesis Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05395)
*Dain Kim,Eungi Cho,Kyumin Kim,Shinyeong Noh,Kyuseong Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KOPA-Bench基准和EDGE方法，通过执行验证的动态图合成工具调用数据，微调9B模型在多步API调用任务上接近27B模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据主权法规要求公共机构部署开源本地LLM代理，但开源模型在多步工具调用场景表现不佳，且缺乏衡量该差距的基准。

Method: 提出KOPA-Bench基准(145个真实任务)和EDGE方法：构建工具输出-输入关系图，通过实际API调用验证有效链接，遍历验证链接合成可执行多步轨迹，使用GRPO微调模型。

Result: 9B模型经EDGE数据微调后，在KOPA-Bench和BFCL基准上性能显著提升，接近同系列未微调27B模型水平。

Conclusion: EDGE方法通过执行驱动的数据合成有效缩小了开源模型在多步工具调用任务上的性能差距，为数据主权场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Data-sovereignty regulations increasingly require public institutions to deploy open-source, on-premise LLM agents that chain multiple tool-calls across live government APIs. However, open-source models consistently underperform in this multi-step setting, and no existing benchmark measures the gap. We introduce the Korean Open Public API Benchmark (KOPA-Bench), comprising 145 real-world tasks. To close this gap, we present EDGE, an Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph for tool-calling data synthEsis driven by live execution. EDGE builds a graph of how each tool's output can feed another's input, keeps only the links that succeed when actually called against the live APIs, and traverses these verified links to synthesize executable multi-step trajectories. Fine-tuned via GRPO on the resulting dataset, our 9B model nearly matches the untuned 27B model from the same family, improving substantially not only on KOPA-Bench but also on the BFCL benchmark.

</details>


### [246] [ACE: Adaptive Calibration-Free Expert Skipping for MoE-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05228)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Jiangyong Yu,Houji Wen,Jun Li,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE，一种无需训练和校准的框架，通过全局谱代理和路由条件细化两个组件，在推理时动态跳过MoE模型中低贡献的专家槽位，显著减少冗余计算并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE架构中固定top-k路由导致每个token激活相同数量的专家，产生大量冗余计算；而现有的专家跳过方法依赖路由置信度、校准数据或额外训练，无法可靠评估专家的实际贡献。

Method: 提出ACE框架，包含两个组件：1) 全局谱代理(GSP)，从耦合的门控、上投影和下投影及RMSNorm缩放中估计全局变换能力；2) 路由条件细化(RCR)，基于中心化的路由权重构建专家特定方向原型，并沿路由偏好方向评估专家响应。推理时结合两者估计，仅当均判定为低贡献且保留top-1专家时跳过专家槽位，所有统计量离线计算，在线仅需查表和轻量标量运算。

Result: 在三个MoE基LLM和八个基准测试上，ACE consistently优于现有静态和动态基线，尤其在激进专家跳过下优势更明显；在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上50%跳过率时，WikiText-2困惑度降低7.96%，平均下游准确率提升4.15个百分点。

Conclusion: ACE作为一种训练免费、校准免费且保留检查点的框架，能高效实现token自适应专家跳过，显著平衡计算效率与模型性能，为MoE模型推理优化提供新范式。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures provide an efficient paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs), yet fixed top-k routing activates the same number of expert slots for every token, causing substantial redundant computation. Existing expert-skipping methods often rely on router confidence, calibration data, or additional training, and therefore cannot reliably estimate the actual contribution of routed experts. To this end, we propose ACE, a training-free, calibration-free, and checkpoint-preserving framework for token-adaptive expert skipping in MoE-based LLMs. ACE contains two complementary components: 1) Global Spectral Proxy (GSP), which estimates global transformation capacity from the coupled gate, up, and down projections together with RMSNorm scaling; and 2) Router-Conditioned Refinement (RCR), which constructs expert-specific direction prototypes from centered router weights and evaluates expert responses along routing-preferred directions. During inference, ACE combines both estimates with runtime router gates and skips an expert slot only when both views identify it as low-contribution, while always retaining the top-1 expert. All expert statistics are computed offline, leaving only table lookups and lightweight scalar operations online. Extensive experiments across three MoE-based LLMs and eight benchmarks demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing static and dynamic baselines, with increasingly pronounced advantages under aggressive expert skipping. For instance, at a 50% skipping ratio on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, ACE reduces WikiText-2 perplexity by 7.96% and improves average downstream accuracy by 4.15 percentage points over the strongest competing method.

</details>


### [247] [Substrate-Aware AI Agents: Execution Context as a First-Class Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05232)
*Manu Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“基底盲视”概念，指AI代理在规划时忽略执行环境约束（如内存、时间）。通过在数值代码生成任务中向三个前沿模型（Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6-Sol, Gemini 3.7 Flash）披露严格的资源契约（128MB/10s 或 96MB），研究发现披露契约能显著促使模型生成结构更优的代码（如原地操作、内存映射），大幅降低内存峰值和运行时间，并显著提高任务在资源限制内的成功率，证明了“基底感知”规划的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI代理在具有严格内存、执行时间和计算约束的环境中日益增多，现有的规划状态往往缺乏对这些执行上下文的感知，导致“基底盲视”（substrate blindness）。这种盲视使得代理生成的计划在实际部署中可能因资源超限而失败。本文旨在通过数值代码生成这一可观察具体实现选择的场景，验证向代理提供执行契约是否能促使其进行主动的结构适应，从而解决基底盲视问题。

Method: 研究选取了三个前沿模型配置（Anthropic Claude Opus 5, OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol, Google Gemini 3.7 Flash），要求它们为高维成对欧几里得距离任务生成代码。实验设置了两组条件：仅仅提供任务描述（task-only）与提供任务描述加上明确的执行契约（contract-disclosed，如128 MB RAM和10.0秒墙钟时间，或更严格的96 MB契约）。通过对比两组条件下生成代码的峰值进程内存（MaxRSS）、墙钟时间（wall time）以及任务成功率，并审计代码结构变化（如阻塞边界、数据类型保留、遍历策略、缓冲区管理），来评估执行契约披露的影响。

Result: 在14组可执行的索引对齐比较中，契约披露使13组的峰值进程内存降低，且所有三个模型 cohort 的平均墙钟时间均减少，执行速度最高提升3.1倍。代码审计显示，披露契约促使模型采用了有界阻塞、float32保留、上三角遍历及原地或内存映射缓冲区等结构性优化。在更严格的96 MB契约下，契约披露组的成功率和资源效率显著优于仅任务组：Claude Opus 5成功率从0/5提升至4/5，GPT-5.6-Sol从1/5提升至5/5，Gemini 3.7 Flash从0/5提升至3/5；同时，披露组的平均MaxRSS和墙钟时间分别比仅任务组低49-74%和35-64%。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了“基底感知”代理规划的概念验证：最小化的执行契约能够诱导生成程序发生主动的结构性适应，将计算从无约束的分配转向受控策略，从而在执行前显著改善观察到的资源 - 时间profile。这表明将执行上下文纳入规划状态是解决自主AI代理在受限环境中有效运行的关键。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly select actions in environments whose memory, execution-time, runtime, compute, and operational constraints determine what counts as a suitable plan. We call the absence of this execution context from an agent's planning state substrate blindness. We test this general proposition through numerical code generation, where selected implementation choices and operational consequences are directly observable. Three frontier model configurations--Anthropic Claude Opus 5, OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol, and Google Gemini 3.7 Flash--generate code for a high-dimensional pairwise Euclidean-distance task either from the task alone or with a 128 MB RAM and 10.0 s wall-time contract. Contract disclosure reduced measured peak process memory in 13 of 14 executable index-aligned task-only versus contract-disclosed comparisons and reduced mean wall time in all three cohorts, making execution up to 3.1x faster. Across the audited corpus, disclosure produced structural code changes including bounded blocking, float32 retention, upper-triangle traversal, and in-place or memory-mapped buffers. At a tighter 96 MB contract, independently sampled contract-disclosed cohorts achieved correct-and-within-budget outcomes of 4/5 for Claude Opus 5, 5/5 for GPT-5.6-Sol, and 3/5 for Gemini 3.7 Flash, compared with task-only outcomes of 0/5, 1/5, and 0/5; cohort mean MaxRSS and wall time were 49-74% and 35-64% lower than their task-only references. These results establish a controlled proof of concept for substrate-aware agent planning: a minimal execution contract induces proactive structural adaptation in generated programs, shifting computation away from unconstrained allocations and substantially improving observed resource-time profiles before execution.

</details>


### [248] [Uncensored Open-weight Models: Redistribution as the Persistence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05241)
*10a Labs,:,Juliette Garcia,Hailey May,Bobby McKenzie,David Pham,Matthew Swain,Joshua Valdez,Corie Wieland,Zachary Yahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了2024年1月至2026年3月期间，开源AI模型安全护栏被移除的生态系统，发现3471个原始无审查模型被平均重新打包2.4次，其中25%的GitHub应用被归类为恶意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着开放权重AI模型生态系统的快速扩张，越来越多的参与者正在移除内置的安全护栏，本文旨在分析这一生态系统的结构、关键参与者及其潜在风险。

Method: 通过识别HuggingFace上的原始无审查模型及其下游重新分发情况，分析模型的量化、镜像和部署情况，并统计GitHub上集成无审查大语言模型(ULLMs)的应用及其恶意比例。

Result: 识别出3471个原始无审查模型，每个模型平均被重新打包2.4次，三个主要参与者占所有8164次压缩重新分发的52%；在1643个集成ULLMs的GitHub应用中，25%被明确归类为恶意。

Conclusion: 无审查AI模型一旦被量化和镜像，即使上游移除也难以消除，且更容易在下游部署，其中相当比例的应用具有恶意性质，表明该生态系统存在显著安全风险。

Abstract: A rapidly expanding ecosystem of actors is removing built-in safety guardrails from open-weight AI models. We profile this ecosystem by identifying key producers, downstream reproductions, and emerging applications. Between January 2024 and March 2026, we identified 3,471 original uncensored models on HuggingFace, each repackaged an average of 2.4 times; three actors account for 52% of all 8,164 compressed redistributions. Once quantized and mirrored across separate accounts, formats, and registries such as Ollama, these models persist regardless of upstream removal and become easier to deploy downstream. Of the 1,643 identified GitHub applications integrating uncensored large language models (ULLMs), 25% were classified as explicitly malicious.

</details>


### [249] [Do LLMs Exhibit Coherent Knowledge Structures in Mathematical Reasoning? A Perspective from Knowledge Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05245)
*Peng Cui,Heejin Do,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于知识空间理论（KST）的框架，评估大语言模型（LLM）在数学推理中的知识结构。研究发现，LLM 不符合人类知识结构，经常违反知识依赖关系，且不同 LLM 之间的知识结构也不一致，这些缺陷在基于准确率的评估中难以被发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在复杂推理任务上表现优异，但尚不清楚它们是否具备连贯的、类似人类的知识结构。人类知识本质上是结构化和相互依赖的，掌握一个概念需要先掌握其先决条件，这一原则由知识空间理论（KST）形式化。因此，需要评估 LLM 的行为是否符合原则性的知识依赖关系。

Method: 引入一个基于知识空间理论（KST）的框架，用于评估 LLM 在数学推理中的知识结构。该框架作为规范性框架，分析 LLM 行为是否遵循原则性的知识依赖关系。研究评估了八个开源和闭源 LLM，并将其与真实人类学习者的表现进行对比。

Result: 研究发现：（1）LLM 不符合人类知识结构，经常违反知识依赖关系，且无法利用上下文中提供的相关知识来提高对依赖问题的表现；（2）LLM 之间没有一致的知识结构，其知识分布的重叠度较低。此外，这些结构性缺陷在基于准确率的评估和 LLM 作为评判者的评估中 largely invisible（大部分不可见）。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了行为证据，表明当前大语言模型的知识结构不遵循类似人类的结构。这意味着仅依靠准确率等指标评估 LLM 可能无法揭示其知识结构的缺陷，需要更细致的评估框架来理解 LLM 的知识组织方式。

Abstract: Human knowledge is inherently structured and interdependent: mastery of a concept requires prior mastery of its prerequisites, a principle formalized by Knowledge Space Theory (KST). While LLMs achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they exhibit coherent, human-like knowledge structure. We introduce a KST-grounded framework for evaluating LLM knowledge structure in mathematical reasoning, using it as a normative framework to analyze whether LLM behavior adheres to principled knowledge dependencies. Evaluating eight open- and closed-source LLMs against real human learners, we find that (1) LLMs do not adhere to human knowledge structure -- they frequently violate knowledge dependencies and fail to leverage related knowledge provided in context to improve performance on dependent questions; (2) LLMs do not share a consistent knowledge structure among themselves, as reflected by low overlap in their knowledge distributions. Furthermore, these structural deficiencies remain largely invisible to accuracy-based and LLM-as-judge evaluations. Together, our results provide behavioral evidence that current LLMs knowledge does not follow a human-like structure.

</details>


### [250] [A Unified Physics-Aware Quantum Machine Learning Framework across Power GaN HEMTs and Logic Nanowire FETs: Predicting Unseen Process Splits and Held-Out Geometry Combinations with Lower Error and Tighter Split-to-Split Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05251)
*Rushat Rai,Yun-Yuan Wang,Autsada Kakaen,Pei-Jie Chang,Doan Viet Nguyen,Yuan-Chieh Chiu,Doldet Tantraviwat,Niall Tumilty,Simon See,Wen-Jay Lee,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Tian-Li Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的统一框架，用于发现紧凑的参数化量子电路（PQCs），以解决数据稀缺的设备建模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺的情况下，传统的设备建模方法难以准确预测设备性能，且缺乏物理一致性。需要一种能够自动发现高效电路架构的方法，以降低建模误差并提高泛化能力。

Method: 使用图神经网络（GNN）作为策略，通过近端策略优化（PPO）进行优化，利用留一法交叉验证（LOGOCV）误差作为奖励信号来搜索电路架构。

Result: 在11个目标上实现了最低的平均绝对误差（MAE），相比六个经典基线方法，HEMTs的Ioff误差降低59%，VTH折叠变异性降低81%；NWFETs的VTH、SS、Ioff误差降低84%，Ioff折叠变异性降低82%。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了RL选择的、经典模拟的PQCs作为紧凑代理模型的潜力，具有低分布外（OOD）误差和改进的物理一致性，且无需显式物理约束或设备特定方程。

Abstract: We present a unified reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that discovers compact parametrized quantum circuits (PQCs) for data-scarce device modeling. A graph neural network (GNN) policy optimized by proximal policy optimization (PPO) searches circuit architectures using leave-one-group-out cross-validation (LOGOCV) error on held-out process or geometry groups as the reward. The framework achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) on all 11 targets versus six classical baselines, with 59% lower error (Ioff) and 81% tighter fold variability (VTH) for HEMTs and 84% lower error (VTH, SS, Ioff) and 82% tighter fold variability (Ioff) for NWFETs. These results demonstrate the potential of RL-selected, classically simulated PQCs as compact surrogates with low OOD error and improved physical consistency, despite imposing no explicit physical constraints, penalty terms, or device-specific equations, on the two evaluated device datasets.

</details>


### [251] [Commonsense Reasoning in Computer Vision: Foundations, Recent Advancements, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05257)
*Bahar Uddin Mahmud,Sumit Barua,Guan Yue Hong,Ajay Gupta,Hexu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了将常识知识融入计算机视觉任务的最新进展，涵盖知识图谱、场景图、神经符号模型和常识增强Transformer等方法，并指出当前局限性与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN模型仅能识别图像中的对象，缺乏对场景的整体理解与推理能力；引入常识知识可提升模型对物体关系、动作及上下文因素的理解，从而实现更精准的现实世界预测与人机交互。

Method: 系统性综述方法：梳理基于知识图谱、场景图、神经符号模型及常识增强Transformer的常识融合技术，分析其在视觉任务中的应用机制。

Result: 总结了当前常识与视觉融合的主要技术路径，识别出数据集偏差、知识不完整性和集成挑战等关键限制因素。

Conclusion: 未来研究应聚焦跨模态推理、可扩展常识注入及神经符号混合架构，以构建真正智能的视觉系统。

Abstract: Commonsense reasoning in computer vision encompasses integrating visual data and contextual knowledge, crucial for enhancing AI's understanding of everyday scenarios. This understanding not only improves machine learning models but also enhances their ability to interact meaningfully with humans and the environment. Unlike CNN-based conventional vision models, which are designed to identify objects within a specific image, incorporating commonsense knowledge enables models to interpret scenes in a more holistic manner, thereby improving their spatial ability to reason about relationships among objects and actions. This integration not only enhances object recognition but also facilitates a deeper understanding of the contextual factors, ultimately leading to more precise predictions and interactions in real-world applications. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of recent developments that integrate commonsense knowledge into computer vision tasks. We systematically review approaches based on knowledge graphs, scene graphs, neuro-symbolic models, and commonsense-augmented transformers. We also outline current limitations related to dataset bias, knowledge incompleteness, and integration challenges. Finally, we highlight prospective research trajectories in cross-modal reasoning, scalable commonsense knowledge injection, and neuro-symbolic hybrid architectures to develop truly intelligent visual systems.

</details>


### [252] [Trace2Tower: Transition-Aware EigenTrace Induction of Multi-Level Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05261)
*Jiazheng Sun,Boyu Yang,Binhao Yuan,Mingxuan Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Trace2Tower框架，通过转换感知的特征轨迹分解，将原始执行轨迹提炼为鲁棒的技能层级结构，显著提升了大语言模型代理在复杂交互任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型代理依赖执行轨迹学习复杂任务时，受限于浅层的轨迹检索和平面的技能总结，根本性地忽略了代理行为中的时间依赖性和结果条件的拓扑结构。

Method: 引入Trace2Tower框架，包含：1) 将步级交互抽象为规范事件，构建由语义兼容性、转移动力学和结果证据控制的统一图；2) 利用新颖的对比谱分解技术，隔离稳定且与成功对齐的行为模式，抑制易失败的捷径；3) 将这些模式有机填充到动态“技能塔”中（包含动作模板、程序例程和总体任务策略），并通过验证器引导的反馈持续优化。

Result: 在ALFWorld基准上达到87.31%成功率（仅需10.35步，0.26次无效动作）；在WebShop基准上达到50.67%的精确成功率。在任务掌握度和上下文高效经验复用方面显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: Trace2Tower通过构建基于时间依赖和结果导向的技能层级，有效解决了现有范式在轨迹利用上的瓶颈，实现了更高效、更鲁棒的代理任务执行能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on execution traces to master complex interactive tasks. However, current paradigms are bottlenecked by shallow trajectory retrieval and flat skill summarization, fundamentally ignoring the temporal dependencies and outcome-conditioned topology of agent behavior. We introduce Trace2Tower, a transition-aware EigenTrace framework that distills raw trajectories into a robust skill hierarchy. Trace2Tower abstracts step-level interactions into canonical events, constructing a unified graph governed by semantic compatibility, transition dynamics, and outcome evidence. Through a novel contrastive spectral decomposition, it isolates stable, success-aligned behavioral modes while rigorously suppressing failure-prone shortcuts. These modes organically populate a dynamic skill tower of action templates, procedural routines, and overarching task strategies, continuously refined via verifier-guided feedback. On ALFWorld, Trace2Tower achieves 87.31% success requiring only 10.35 steps and 0.26 invalid actions; on WebShop, it reaches 50.67% exact success. Across both benchmarks, Trace2Tower significantly outperforms existing baselines in task mastery and context-efficient experience reuse.

</details>


### [253] [AI for Computational Design Science: A Responsible Human-AI Framework and Case Study on Short-Form Video Safety Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05270)
*Wenli Zhang,Jiaheng Xie,Zhihe Pan,Yidong Chai,Xiao Fang,Sudha Ram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI4CDS框架，将AI融入计算设计科学，并通过ChildRiskGuard实例验证了其在儿童视频风险检测中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对AI积极参与问题构建、资源建设、设计搜索、评估和知识抽象的计算设计科学（CDS）缺乏指导，需要一种新的方法论框架来规范AI在CDS中的角色与责任。

Method: 开发了AI4CDS五阶段方法论框架，强调AI扩展搜索能力而研究人员保留领域基础、可接受性、验证和科学判断责任；通过ChildRiskGuard实例化该框架，构建可解释的儿童短视频风险检测工具。

Result: ChildRiskGuard在检测儿童不适宜短视频任务中F1分数达0.769，显著优于通用内容安全模型的直接应用，且与强基准保持竞争力；成功分离通用与儿童特定风险，实现概念级解释嵌入预测计算。

Conclusion: AI4CDS为AI赋能的计算设计科学提供了负责任的方法论框架，ChildRiskGuard证明了AI扩展、研究者主导的设计能生成和评估新型计算设计知识。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is transforming not only what information systems researchers design, but also how design research is conducted. Yet existing literature offers limited guidance for computational design science (CDS) when AI actively participates in problem formulation, resource construction, design search, evaluation, and knowledge abstraction. We develop AI for Computational Design Science (AI4CDS), a five-phase methodological framework in which AI expands problem and design search while researchers retain responsibility for domain grounding, admissibility, verification, and scientific judgment. Collaboration is governed by graduated trust, reversibility, auditability, and differentiated reproducibility. We instantiate AI4CDS through ChildRiskGuard, an interpretable artifact for detecting short-form videos inappropriate for children, while documenting AI interactions, rejected alternatives, corrections, and audit trails. The case translates audience-dependent safety and explanation faithfulness into three technical challenges and develops an artifact that separates generic from child-specific risk, represents distinct developmental-risk mechanisms, and makes concept-level explanations part of the predictive computation. ChildRiskGuard achieves an F1 score of 0.769, substantially outperforming direct application of a general-purpose content-safety model while remaining competitive with strong benchmarks. The primary contribution is AI4CDS as a responsible framework for AI-enabled CDS; ChildRiskGuard provides process and artifact evidence of how AI-expanded, researcher-governed design can generate and evaluate novel computational design knowledge.

</details>


### [254] [Don't Drop Dropout: Optimizing Layer Sparsity for Efficient LLM Training and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05275)
*Mostafa Elhoushi,Alex Pretko,Nolan Dey,Bin Claire Zhang,Gavia Gray,Gurpreet Gosal,Abdulrahman Mahmoud,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，在大型语言模型（LLM）预训练中，通过优化层丢弃（Layer Dropout）的分布、时间调度及超参数，可以在同等训练计算量下降低损失，或在同等训练步数下节省高达25%的计算量，同时支持推理阶段的加速优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管层丢弃在Transformer模型中被证明能加速训练并提高鲁棒性，但在大规模LLM预训练中已逐渐被弃用，且缺乏对其精度下降影响的量化研究与缓解方案。

Method: 通过超过2400次训练实验，覆盖2.71亿至82亿参数模型及高达1600亿token的数据集，系统分析层丢弃的最佳分布、时间调度及优化器超参数配置，并在Cerebras CS-3系统上进行验证。

Result: 在相同训练FLOPs下层丢弃能降低损失；在固定训练步数下可节省高达25%训练FLOPs且验证损失相当或更低；支持早退、中间层跳过等推理优化，实现高达1.5倍推理加速且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 层丢弃应重新应用于最先进LLM训练中，通过优化配置可实现训练效率与推理性能的双重提升，且该结论在大规模训练场景下具有可靠性。

Abstract: Layer dropout (a.k.a. stochastic depth) has been shown to enable faster training, higher accuracy, and robustness to zero-shot layer pruning in both language and vision transformers. However, as models and datasets have scaled, dropout - particularly layer dropout - has largely disappeared from large language models (LLMs) pre-training recipes. While some prior work has reported that dropout can degrade accuracy, no comprehensive study has quantified, let alone mitigated, this effect. In this study, we show that layer dropout should be used in state-of-the-art LLM training, establishing best practices and scaling analysis for both training and post-training benefits. Concretely, with optimal layer distribution, time schedule, and optimizer hyperparameters, we observe that at the same training FLOPs layer dropout leads to lower loss. For a given number of training steps, LLMs can achieve lower or similar validation loss while saving upto 25% of training FLOPs. Moreover, layer dropout enables significant post-training optimizations, such as early exit, intermediate-layer skipping, and self-speculative decoding, yielding up to 1.5x inference speedup with negligible accuracy loss. Across more than 2400 training experiments, spanning models from 271M to 8.2B parameters and datasets up to 160B tokens, we demonstrate that these findings extend reliably to large-scale training regimes. All pre-training experiments were run on Cerebras CS-3 systems.

</details>


### [255] [Testing Interchangeability in LLM Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05279)
*Jianxin Gao,Tianyi Yu,Linna Deng,Runze Li,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了多智能体系统中角色可完全互换的假设，发现虽然替换智能体对任务得分影响较小，但会显著增加沟通成本（16%-63%），且这种“替换惩罚”与团队独立演化产生的分歧程度正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证生产级多智能体系统中“填补同一角色的智能体是完全可互换的”这一普遍假设是否成立，特别是探究智能体替换对任务表现和协调效率的具体影响。

Method: 基于同一基础模型独立构建8个团队，让智能体在10次组建过程中积累私有笔记；随后在不同团队间交换角色匹配的智能体，并在保留测试任务上测量变化；设置安慰剂对照组以区分人员变动与单纯的名册干扰；通过改变基础模型、解码温度和组建长度进行消融实验。

Result: 智能体替换对任务得分影响微小，但使单位进度的沟通成本增加16%至63%；在Hanabi中，替换有经验的智能体比使用新手成本更高，表明存在旧伙伴习得惯例的干扰；在Collab-Overcooked中，议程设定者被替换后，额外沟通主要来自留守智能体；替换惩罚程度与独立团队间的分歧漂移程度同步变化（贪婪解码降低两者，增加历史长度则提高两者）。

Conclusion: 在这些设定下，智能体在任务结果上具有较高的可替代性，但在协调效率上可替代性较低；团队形成历史越长，独立演化导致的分歧越大，替换智能体带来的协调成本惩罚也越显著。

Abstract: Production multi-agent systems replace agents constantly, on the assumption that an agent filling a role is interchangeable with any other agent that can do the job. We test that assumption. Eight teams per setting are formed independently from one base model on the same tasks, each agent keeping a private notebook across ten formation episodes; we then trade role-matched agents between teams and measure what changes on held-out tasks. Against a placebo that reproduces the disruption of a roster change without changing who occupies the seat, a swap costs little in task score but raises the communication a team spends per unit of progress by 16 to 63 percent, and in Hanabi a swapped agent is more expensive than an inexperienced one, consistent with interference from conventions learned with its former partner. In Collab-Overcooked, when the agent that sets the agenda is replaced, most of the extra communication comes from the agent that stayed. Three ablations, over base models, decoding temperature and formation length, move the swap penalty alongside one other quantity: how far independently formed teams drift apart. Greedy decoding lowers both; doubling a team's history raises both. In these settings, agents are more fungible in task outcome than in coordination efficiency, with larger swap effects after longer formation histories.

</details>


### [256] [GUT: Quantifying and Optimizing the Reasoning Uncertainty of LLMs via Graph Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05284)
*Shuang Liang,Xin-Yu Hu,Xiang-Jun Ou,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GUT方法，通过图复杂度量化并优化大语言模型推理过程中的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理过程存在不确定性，同一输入下会产生发散分支，部分推理链荒谬。

Method: 构建有向无环图覆盖推理分支，包含量化模块(GUT-Q)和优化模块(GUT-O)。GUT-Q用图复杂度近似推理空间复杂度，GUT-O将负不确定性作为强化学习奖励函数。

Result: 在4个大语言模型和5个数据集上的实验验证了GUT的有效性。

Conclusion: GUT方法能有效量化并降低大语言模型推理不确定性。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed great advances in the reasoning ability of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the reasoning processes of LLMs often exhibit uncertainty, where LLMs often produce a proliferation of divergent branches at each reasoning step even when fed the same prompting inputs, and certain branches exhibit evidently incredible, even nonsensical, reasoning chains and results. In this paper, we propose the Graph-complexity-based UncerTainty (GUT) method for investigating the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs. The key idea of GUT is to characterize the potential branches of each reasoning chain with a directed acyclic graph, thereby ensuring that all potential branches are comprehensively covered within the graph space. Building upon this recognition, we further build two modules of GUT, that is, a Quantification (GUT-Q) module and an Optimization (GUT-O) module, for quantifying and reducing the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs, respectively. GUT-Q measures LLM reasoning uncertainty by approximating the reasoning space complexity with graph complexity. GUT-O implements uncertainty optimization by treating negative uncertainty as the reward function in reinforcement learning. Experimental results conducted on four LLMs and five datasets validate the effectiveness of GUT.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond Aggregate Scores: Behavioral Correctness Assumptions for Assessing Reference-Based Automatic Evaluation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05289)
*Maria Mahbub,Ashley Rice,Michael R. Munroe,Amidu Kamara,Amir Sadovnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“行为正确性假设”框架，通过控制响应变换来评估自动评估方法的行为特性，发现不同评估器存在显著的行为权衡，且传统聚合指标无法揭示这些差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元评估主要关注与人类判断或基准标签的一致性，缺乏在受控条件下对评估器行为特性的深入洞察，无法揭示评估方法在具体行为假设下的表现差异。

Method: 定义保持正确性和改变正确性的假设分类体系，通过控制响应变换操作化这些假设，系统评估词汇级、字符级、语义级、大语言模型基于及混合式评估器在假设层面的行为、稳定性、敏感性、重复运行变异性、配置敏感性和可复现性。

Result: 实验表明，不同评估范式存在明显的行为权衡：无任何评估器能满足所有提出的正确性假设；具有相似聚合性能的评估器可能表现出截然不同的行为特征。

Conclusion: 行为正确性假设框架能提供传统聚合元评估所掩盖的诊断信息，为理解和改进自然语言生成系统的自动评估方法提供新视角。

Abstract: Automated reference-based evaluation methods play a critical role in assessing natural language generation systems. Existing meta-evaluation primarily measures agreement with human judgments or benchmark labels, providing limited insight into evaluator behavior under controlled conditions. We introduce behavioral correctness assumptions, a complementary framework for evaluating reference-based automatic evaluation methods. We define a taxonomy of correctness-preserving and correctness-altering assumptions and operationalize them through controlled response transformations that specify expected scoring behaviors. We evaluate diverse lexical, character-level, semantic, LLM-based, and hybrid evaluators and analyze their assumption-level behavior, stability, sensitivity, repeat-run variability, configuration sensitivity, and reproducibility. Our experiments reveal distinct behavioral trade-offs across evaluation paradigms: no evaluator satisfies all proposed correctness assumptions, and evaluators with similar aggregate performance can exhibit substantially different behavioral profiles. These findings demonstrate that behavioral correctness assumptions provide diagnostic information obscured by conventional aggregate meta-evaluation.

</details>


### [258] [RISE: Recursive Improvement via Self-Extrapolating Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05295)
*Yang Li,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RISE 提出了一种基于模型自身 RLVR 训练轨迹构建合成教师的递归自外推策略蒸馏方法，通过将稀疏的结果奖励转化为密集的 token 级目标，在数学推理、STEM、代码生成等任务上优于纯 RLVR 和现有自蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 On-policy Distillation (OPD) 受限于教师模型质量：外部教师存在分布不匹配问题，而基于特权条件的自蒸馏受限于上下文学习能力。需要一种无需外部模型或特权条件，且能持续改进的蒸馏机制。

Method: RISE (Recursive Improvement via Self-Extrapolating Policy Distillation) 从模型自身的 RLVR 训练轨迹中构建合成教师。通过在参数空间或输出 logit 空间中外推当前检查点与滞后锚点之间的位移，将稀疏的结果诱导参数更新转化为密集的 token 级目标。RISE 将 RLVR 与 OPD 结合形成互补循环，并在每次迭代中刷新教师模型。

Result: 在数学推理、多领域 STEM、代码生成和多轮智能体任务上的实验表明，RISE 在所有设置中均优于仅使用 RLVR 的训练方法和现有的 on-policy 自蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: RISE 通过将稀疏奖励转化为密集监督信号，并利用递归改进机制，有效解决了 OPD 中教师质量瓶颈问题，实现了无需外部依赖的持续性能提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense, per-token supervision for language model post-training, but its effectiveness is bottlenecked by teacher quality: external teachers suffer from distribution mismatch, while self-distillation with privileged conditioning is limited by in-context learning capacity. We propose \textbf{RISE} (\textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{I}mprovement via \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{E}xtrapolating Policy Distillation), which constructs a synthetic teacher directly from the model's own RLVR training trajectory. By extrapolating the displacement between the current checkpoint and a trailing anchor---in parameter space or output logit space---RISE converts a sparse outcome-induced parameter update into a dense token-level target, without any external model or privileged conditioning. RISE combines RLVR and OPD in a complementary loop: outcome rewards ground the extrapolation toward correct reasoning, while the extrapolated teacher refines token-level decisions. Moreover, since the teacher is refreshed every iteration as the student improves, distillation becomes a recursive improvement mechanism rather than a one-shot compression step. Experiments spanning mathematical reasoning, multi-domain STEM, code generation, and multi-turn agentic tasks show that RISE outperforms RLVR-only training and on-policy self-distillation across all settings.

</details>


### [259] [LLM-Driven Algorithm Design for Quantum Circuit Synthesis based on Binary Decision Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05327)
*Yoonju Sim,Federico Berto,Chuanbo Hua,Jinkyoo Park,Changhyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuantumEvo框架，利用LLM作为启发式生成器，优化BDD变量排序以降低量子电路成本（QCC），新启发式HGA-QE在基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BDD变量排序启发式方法主要最小化BDD大小，但BDD大小不能准确反映合成电路的量子成本（QCC），需要更直接针对QCC优化的方法。

Method: 提出QuantumEvo进化框架，使用LLM生成启发式规则，搜索多种启发式家族初始化的排序启发式，候选启发式通过标准BDD操作直接修改变量排序，并由下游QCC选择。

Result: 发现的启发式HGA-QE修改遗传算法中的sifting步骤以更好对齐QCC，在基准集上实现70.9%的平局或胜率，13.5%的函数上严格最优，在不同来源的基准套件上显示明显优势。

Conclusion: QuantumEvo框架能有效优化BDD变量排序以降低量子电路成本，HGA-QE启发式在多种基准测试中表现具有竞争力，尤其在严格胜利方面优势明显。

Abstract: Quantum circuits are central to implementing quantum algorithms on quantum devices, where quantum gates must be reversible. Many quantum algorithms rely on Boolean functions, which must therefore be implemented reversibly within quantum circuits. Reversible circuit synthesis provides a way to translate such Boolean functions into reversible circuits. Binary decision diagrams (BDDs) offer a scalable approach to this task, but the resulting BDDs and circuits depend heavily on variable ordering. Existing ordering heuristics commonly minimize BDD size because it is closely tied to the circuit size. However, BDD size is an imperfect proxy for the quantum cost of the synthesized circuit (QCC). We propose \texttt{QuantumEvo}, an evolutionary framework that uses an LLM as a heuristic generator for QCC-aware BDD variable ordering. Instead of predicting orderings directly, \texttt{QuantumEvo} searches over ordering heuristics initialized from multiple heuristic families. Candidate heuristics directly manipulate variable orderings using standard BDD operations and are selected by downstream QCC. The discovered heuristic, HGA-QE, modifies the sifting step inside a genetic algorithm so that the procedure is better aligned with QCC. Across the benchmark set, HGA-QE achieves a 70.9\% tie-or-win rate against the per-function best baseline and is strictly best on 13.5\% of the functions. The results demonstrate broadly competitive QCC performance, with HGA-QE showing a clearer relative advantage in strict wins on the two benchmark suites drawn from sources different from the data used for heuristic discovery.

</details>


### [260] [Who Should Grade My Work? Student Perspectives on Transparent AI-Assisted Writing Assessment in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05346)
*Rayed AlGhamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了学生在明确知晓反馈由AI生成时的反应，发现学生虽认可GenAI反馈的实用性，但坚持认为人类教师才拥有评分的权威，提出了“反馈效用”与“评估权威”相分离的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注GenAI反馈是否提升写作表现及其感知有用性，但鲜有研究探讨当学生明确被告知评分和反馈由AI而非人类教师生成时，他们如何解读和回应这种评估。

Method: 在沙特一所公立大学的本科技术传播课程中进行定性教学探究。13名男性计算专业学生完成手写写作任务，其扫描件由ChatGPT基于量规提示进行评分和反馈。学生被告知真相后撰写反思，研究者对这些反思进行了归纳式主题分析。

Result: 分析识别出四个主题：反馈的感知有用性、对AI情境与教学局限性的认知、条件性信任（区分反馈效用与评估权威）、以及对人类教师制度与教学角色的反思。学生接受GenAI用于表面修改，但一致将评分决定权归于人类教师。

Conclusion: 学生将“反馈效用”与“评估权威”视为分析上分离的两个判断，而非单一认可尺度的两端。这表明在高等教育评估中整合GenAI时，需明确界定AI作为辅助工具与人类作为权威评估者的不同角色。

Abstract: The integration of GenAI tools into higher education assessment raises important questions about how students understand, interpret, and respond to AI-mediated evaluation. As instructors increasingly explore AI tools for providing feedback, prior research has examined whether GenAI-generated feedback improves writing performance and how students perceive its usefulness; comparatively little is known, however, about how students interpret such evaluation when they are explicitly informed that an AI system, rather than a human instructor, produced the feedback and the score. This study reports findings from a qualitative pedagogical inquiry conducted in an undergraduate technical communication course for computing students at a Saudi public university. Thirteen male undergraduate computing students completed an in-class handwritten writing task; the scanned submissions were evaluated by ChatGPT using a rubric-based prompt aligned with the task objectives. Students were then explicitly informed that ChatGPT had generated the score and feedback and were invited to reflect on the evaluation in writing. Inductive thematic analysis of these reflections identified four themes: perceived usefulness of feedback; awareness of AI's contextual and pedagogical limitations; conditional trust, distinguishing feedback utility from evaluative authority; and reflection on the institutional and pedagogical role of the human instructor. Participants accepted GenAI feedback as useful for surface-level revision but consistently positioned the human instructor as the appropriate authority over grading decisions. The study identifies this as a distinction between feedback utility and evaluative authority, two judgments that students treat as analytically separate rather than as opposite ends of a single approval scale...

</details>


### [261] [CUA-Universe: A Scalable and Dynamic Environment for Hybrid GUI+CLI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05374)
*Haoting Shi,Wenhao Wang,Weicheng Fang,Yaozhong Liang,Tian Jin,Pengxiang Zhao,Guangyi Liu,Siheng Chen,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CUA-Universe，一个可扩展的流水线，旨在将真实桌面软件转化为混合GUI+CLI环境，解决了现有智能体在图形界面和命令行操作之间协调不足的问题，显著提升了智能体的成功率和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机使用智能体主要依赖GUI，导致执行轨迹效率低下。现实世界的计算机工作是混合模式（视觉检查+命令行操作），但缺乏可扩展的混合环境支持，且现有智能体难以互补地利用两种接口（CLI智能体缺乏视觉感知，GUI智能体执行命令效率低）。

Method: 提出CUA-Universe流水线，包含三个核心组件：1) App-Forge：将应用适配为可复现的VM并暴露命令行接口；2) Task-Weave：基于种子文件合成可控难度的多样化混合任务；3) Path-Steer：引导 rollout 沿高效混合路径执行并收集验证轨迹用于后训练。

Result: 训练的9B模型在多个基准上表现显著提升：CUA-Verse（分数+39.3，步数-37%，token数-60%）、OSWorld（成功率+16.8，步数-57%，token数-44%）以及OSWorld-MCP（分数+7.84，步数-27%，token数-30%）。

Conclusion: CUA-Universe提供了一条可扩展的路径，能够培养出更强大且高效的计算机使用智能体，实现了从低效GUI交互和脆弱CLI脚本向有效GUI+CLI协同操作的转变。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have advanced on benchmarks like OSWorld and AndroidWorld, but still act mostly through the GUI, often producing inefficient trajectories. Real-world computer work is hybrid, combining visual-state inspection with precise, high-throughput command-line operations, so capable agents must coordinate both modalities over shared application state. Yet scalable hybrid environments remain scarce because supporting both GUI and CLI over real applications typically requires substantial manual engineering for each application. Existing agents also struggle to use the two interfaces complementarily: CLI-native agents lack visual perception for tasks involving interface state or layout, while GUI-native agents are inefficient for operations better executed through commands. We introduce CUA-Universe, a scalable environment-to-data pipeline that turns real desktop software into hybrid GUI+CLI environments. App-Forge adapts applications into reproducible VMs and command-line surfaces it discovers, wraps, or generates, scaling to 16 applications; Task-Weave synthesizes diverse hybrid tasks of controllable difficulty from reusable operations over seed files; and Path-Steer steers rollouts along efficient hybrid paths and harvests verified trajectories for post-training. Training on this data shifts behavior from inefficient GUI interaction and brittle CLI scripting toward effective GUI+CLI orchestration. Our 9B model improves both success and efficiency on CUA-Verse (Score +39.3 pts; -37% steps, -60% tokens), OSWorld (SR +16.8 pts; -57% steps, -44% tokens), and OSWorld-MCP (Score +7.84 pts; -27% steps, -30% tokens). CUA-Universe provides a scalable path toward more capable and efficient computer-use agents.

</details>


### [262] [Molecular Déjà Vu: Digit-Level Retrieval of Published Values in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05381)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Christian J. Cyron,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了22个前沿大语言模型在12个分子性质回归基准测试中的逐字检索行为，发现检索现象普遍但具有基准特异性；推理层级会显著影响检索检出率；抑制检索后模型间的预测误差差异缩小，表明模型的通用预测能力不仅取决于记忆数据的多少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在分子性质基准测试中的准确率无法区分模型是真正预测了性质还是仅仅检索了已发表的数值，导致评估结果存在误导性，需要量化和深入分析这种逐字检索行为的范围、深度及其对评估的影响。

Method: 对22个前沿模型在12个回归基准上进行审计，检测逐字检索现象；在两个不同的推理层级下运行实验以观察推理对检索的影响；在最严重的污染案例中测试中断检索的方法，并分析抑制检索前后模型预测误差分布的变化。

Result: 逐字检索现象广泛存在但具有数据集特异性（5个数据集中超过50%的模型表现出检索行为）；提高推理层级使检索标记率增加了89%；即使经过变换的SMILES字符串和原始标签组合，最强模型仍能识别；抑制检索后，不同模型的相对预测误差趋于一致，而检索行为的差异原本拉大了模型间的差距。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在分子回归基准中的通用预测能力并非仅由记忆数值的多少决定；逐字检索行为普遍且受推理深度影响，现有的基准测试需考虑检索干扰以准确评估模型的真实预测能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on molecular property benchmarks, but accuracy cannot distinguish a model that predicts a property from one that retrieves a published number. We audit 22 frontier models on 12 regression benchmarks for verbatim retrieval and find that it is widespread but relatively benchmark-specific: on five datasets more than $50\%$ of the LLMs show verbatim retrieval, while on the remaining datasets it appears only in isolated cells. We run our experiments at two reasoning levels and find that reasoning changes retrieval. The same experiments, on the same molecules and with the same prompt, are flagged $89\%$ more often at the higher reasoning level than at the lowest one. Finally, we test a way to interrupt retrieval in our most contaminated cases, and find that the strongest models in some cases still recognise a combination of transformed SMILES strings and original labels. Furthermore, suppressing retrieval moves the prediction errors of the different models closer together in relative terms, while their differing use of verbatim retrieval spreads them apart. This indicates that the general predictive capability of an LLM is not determined solely by the amount of memorised values. This work provides an overview of the amount and depth of verbatim retrieval in molecular regression benchmarks using LLMs.

</details>


### [263] [Necessary or Sufficient? Evaluating LLM Explanations With Behavioural Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05385)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Nabeel Kemal,Ashwin Kandath,Owen O'Neill,Guillaume Bourgeon,Houssem Chatbri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了大语言模型（LLM）在代理工作流中提供的解释（即影响决策的因素）的可靠性。研究发现，虽然模型列出的前三个因素包含有用信息，但它们并不能可靠地反映对输出具有最强“必要性”或“充分性”影响的因素，表明解释与 observable 决策行为之间存在显著偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代理工作流中，操作员依赖LLM提供的解释因素来监控系统、诊断错误或决定升级输出。这种依赖假设模型提供的解释与其实际可观察的决策行为一致。然而，这一假设尚未得到充分验证，若解释不可靠，可能导致监控失效或错误的诊断。

Method: 研究提出了两种解释评估标准：必要性（改变该因素是否改变输出）和充分性（仅保留该因素是否维持输出）。通过在两个合成用例（推荐顾问和判断提示有害性）中，对来自Claude、GPT和Gemini家族的八个模型进行受控的黑盒干预实验，计算每个因素的必要性和充分性得分，并将其与模型引用的因素排名进行Spearman相关性分析及对比。

Result: 在两个任务中，模型引用的因素排名与实测的必要性和充分性得分之间的Spearman相关系数较低（顾问推荐任务约为0.35，提示监控任务为0.43-0.58）。此外，在顾问推荐任务中，超过57%的情况下，未被引用的因素得分高于被引用的最低分因素；提示监控任务中该比例分别为25.8%和8.9%。

Conclusion: LLM引用的前三个因素虽然包含有用信息，但无法可靠地识别出对决策具有最强实测影响力的三个因素。该研究框架提供了一种针对单个LLM决策的黑盒可靠性检查方法，用于验证代理监督中使用的解释的可信度。

Abstract: LLM decision components that can operate within agent workflows often produce action-relevant recommendations or judgements together with explanations. Operators may use the named factors to monitor a system, diagnose errors, or decide when to escalate an output. Such use assumes that the explanations agree with the component's observable decision behaviour. We test two interpretations of the named factors: necessity, meaning that changing a factor would change the output, and sufficiency, meaning that retaining it while removing other changeable information would preserve the output. We evaluate these interpretations in two synthetic use cases: recommending advisors to clients and judging prompts for harmfulness or risk. Models return an output and the top three factors that most influenced it. Controlled black-box interventions estimate a necessity score for each factor by measuring how often changing it changes the output, and a sufficiency score by measuring how often retaining it preserves the output. Across eight models from the Claude, GPT, and Gemini families, the mean Spearman correlations between the cited ranking and the necessity and sufficiency scores are 0.349 and 0.354 for advisor recommendation, and 0.431 and 0.580 for prompt monitoring. Furthermore, an uncited factor scores above the lowest-scoring cited factor in 57.6% of advisor responses under necessity and 58.1% under sufficiency; the corresponding prompt-monitoring rates are 25.8% and 8.9%. The cited top three contain useful information but do not reliably identify the three factors with the strongest measured influence under necessity or sufficiency. The framework provides a black-box reliability check for explanations used in agent oversight while remaining scoped to individual LLM decisions.

</details>


### [264] [A Deep Generative Model for Synthesizing Labeled Wireless Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05396)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的IIns-GAN方法，用于生成带有位置标签的真实无线信号，以解决真实数据集获取成本高和传统合成方法真实性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线感知中，带有位置标签的无线信号对性能评估和模型训练至关重要，但获取真实数据集面临测量和标注成本高的挑战；传统合成方法依赖环境模型，需要大量超参数调优且真实性不足。

Method: 提出了一种名为Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN) 的新型深度学习方法，用于生成逼真的带标签无线信号。

Result: 在公开UWB数据集上的实验表明，IIns-GAN生成的信号反映了真实测量的物理特性，并显著提升了多种无线感知任务（如距离估计和环境识别）的模型训练效果。

Conclusion: IIns-GAN能够生成适应不同环境场景的真实无线信号，有效解决了数据获取成本高的问题，对无线感知领域的模型训练具有重要价值。

Abstract: Wireless signals with position-related labels are pivotal for both performance evaluation and model training in the realm of wireless sensing. However, acquiring real-world datasets is often challenged by significant measurement and labeling costs. Traditional methods for synthesizing labeled wireless signals typically rely on environmental models, leading to extensive hyper-parameter tuning and inadequate realism for comprehensive model training purposes. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel deep learning (DL)-based method, namely Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN), to generate realistic labeled wireless signals. The generated signals are particularly adaptive to different environment scenarios and well-suited for various model training tasks, including distance estimation and environment identification. We have conducted extensive experiments on public Ultra-Wideband (UWB) datasets to evaluate the realism and utility of the generated signals. The results demonstrate that the signals generated by IIns-GAN mirror the physical characteristics of real-world measurements, and significantly contribute to the improvement of model training in diverse wireless sensing tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Optimization of STAR-RIS Phase and Energy Splitting Coefficients in OTFS-NOMA Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04536)
*Rais. J. Gachaba,Manobendu Sarker,Anirban Bhowal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合STAR-RIS、OTFS和NOMA技术的下行通信框架，并设计了基于深度强化学习（SAC-BSE）的配置方案，以解决高移动性场景下的实时重构难题，实现了快速收敛和在宽速度范围内的稳定速率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在STAR-RIS辅助的OTFS-NOMA框架中，延迟-多普勒域的高移动性使得传统的交替优化算法无法在每个相干间隔内完成实时重构，导致计算负担过重且难以适应快速变化的信道。

Method: 将STAR-RIS的相移和能量分配设计建模为具有闭式最大比传输波束成形和固定NOMA功率分配的非凸和速率最大化问题；提出一种名为Beta-Space Soft Actor-Critic (SAC-BSE)的最大熵深度强化学习代理，通过单次前向传播将观测到的信道实现映射为STAR-RIS配置，从而避免逐间隔重优化。

Result: 仿真显示，该方案收敛迅速；在用户速度变化128倍的范围内，和速率降解限制在约10%；随着发射功率和STAR-RIS元件数量的增加，其性能一致优于仅OTFS、仅NOMA、仅STAR-RIS、固定分配及模式切换等基线方案。

Conclusion: 基于SAC-BSE的深度强化学习方法有效解决了高移动性环境下STAR-RIS实时配置的难题，在保证低计算复杂度的同时，显著提升了系统的和速率性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper considers a downlink communication framework comprising a simultaneously transmitting and reflecting reconfigurable intelligent surface (STAR-RIS)-aided by orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) and non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) technologies. Further, delay-Doppler mobility in such frameworks renders classical alternating optimization impractical for per-coherence interval reconfiguration. To mitigate such issues, the STAR-RIS phase-shift and energy-splitting design is formulated as a constrained, non-convex sum-rate maximization problem with closed-form maximum ratio transmission beamforming and fixed NOMA power allocation. To circumvent the per-interval re-optimization burden, a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach is adopted that maps observed channel realizations to STAR-RIS configurations through a single forward pass. Specifically, Beta-Space Soft Actor-Critic (SAC-BSE), a maximum entropy DRL agent, is proposed. Simulation results, with two NOMA-multiplexed users on each STAR-RIS branch, confirm rapid convergence, limit the sum-rate degradation to roughly 10\% across a 128-fold user-speed range, and yield consistent gains over OTFS-only, NOMA-only, STAR-RIS-only, fixed-split, and mode-switching baselines as transmit power and the number of STAR-RIS elements increase.

</details>


### [266] [Ultra-High Resolution Method for Multipath Within a Co-Delay-Doppler Bin in DFT-P-OCDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04669)
*Mingxuan Han,Weile Zhang,Feifei Gao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种TSUR框架，用于在DFT-P-OCDM系统中解决多径信号落入同一时延 - 多普勒bin时的感知难题，通过两阶段估计实现高分辨率信道重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双选择性信道中，当多个物理路径落入同一时延 - 多普勒bin时，基于导频推断的可分辨时延类数量可能小于实际物理路径数量，导致路径数确定、分数多普勒偏移估计和信道重建困难。

Method: 推导了DPF域中双选择性信道下多径共bin情况的输入输出点对点关系，提出TSUR框架：第一阶段利用导频相位进展估计时延，第二阶段利用泄漏样本估计多普勒和各时延类内路径数。

Result: 仿真结果表明TSUR能分辨同一时延 - 多普勒bin内的同延迟路径，对时延偏移具有鲁棒性，且分数多普勒估计误差低于顺序提取和离网格基线方法。

Conclusion: TSUR框架有效解决了共bin多径感知问题，实现了通信与感知性能的平衡，并通过CRLB分析揭示了导频配置在感知分辨率与通信恢复之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Communication systems can reuse their transmitted signals for sensing without dedicated radar transmissions. For an established DFT-preprocessed orthogonal chirp division multiplexing (DFT-P-OCDM) waveform, this task becomes difficult when several physical paths in a doubly selective channel fall into the same co-delay-Doppler bin. In this case, the number of resolvable delay classes inferred from the pilot may be smaller than the number of physical paths within the co-bin. This mismatch increases the difficulty of path number determination, fractional Doppler offset estimation, and channel reconstruction. We derive a pointwise relationship between the input and output in the DFT preprocessed Fresnel (DPF) domain for doubly selective channels with multiple paths within the co-delay-Doppler bin. Based on this input and output relation, we propose the two stage ultra high resolution (TSUR) framework. The first stage uses pilots of the phase progression to estimate delay, while the second stage uses the leakage samples to estimate the Doppler and the number of paths within each delay class. Furthermore, we derive CRLBs and analyze how the pilot configuration trades sensing resolution and communication recovery. Simulation results demonstrate that TSUR resolves same delay paths within a co-delay-Doppler bin, remains robust to exist delay offsets, and achieves lower fractional Doppler estimation errors than sequential extraction and off-grid baselines.

</details>


### [267] [The Cross-Correlation Distribution of the Niho-Type Decimation $d=4(2^m-1)+1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04683)
*Maosheng Xiong,Haode Yan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文确定了有限域$\mathbb F_{2^{2m}}$上Niho型解$d=4(2^m-1)+1$的互相关分布，通过将问题转化为多项式$f_a(x)=x^7+ax^4+\bar a x^3+1$在$U_{q+1}$上的根数分布，利用特征和与Kloosterman和计算得到显式频率公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互相关问题是序列设计中的经典问题，确定特定Niho型解的互相关分布对于序列设计和编码理论具有重要意义。

Method: 将互相关分布问题转化为多项式根数分布问题，通过归一化四元子集并研究相关预解式，将枚举问题简化为$\mathbb F_q$上的方程，利用特征和、Kloosterman和及Carlitz定理进行计算。

Result: 获得了互相关分布中所有频率的显式公式。

Conclusion: 成功解决了Niho型解$d=4(2^m-1)+1$的互相关分布问题，为序列设计提供了理论支持。

Abstract: The cross-correlation problem is a classical problem in sequence design. In this paper, we determine the cross-correlation distribution of the Niho-type decimation $d=4(2^m-1)+1$ over $\mathbb F_{2^{2m}}$ for every positive integer $m$. With $q=2^m$, this is equivalent to determining the distribution of the number of roots in $U_{q+1}$ of $f_a(x)=x^7+ax^4+\bar a x^3+1$ for $a\in\mathbb F_{q^2}$, where $U_{q+1}=\{x\in\mathbb F_{q^2}:x^{q+1}=1\}$. The main difficulty is to count the four-element subsets of $U_{q+1}$ that may occur as root sets of $f_a$. We normalize such subsets by their product and study the resulting condition through the associated resolvent. This reduces the required enumeration to several equations over $\mathbb F_q$, which are evaluated by character sums and Kloosterman sums. The remaining mixed Kloosterman sum is related to a Kloosterman sum over $\mathbb F_{2^{2m}}$ and is evaluated using a theorem of Carlitz. Consequently, we obtain explicit formulas for all the frequencies in the cross-correlation distribution.

</details>


### [268] [Decoding Error Probability of the Random Code Ensemble over the Erasure Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04688)
*Chin Hei Chan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文给出了擦除信道下所有$(n,M)_q$码在明确、列表和最大似然解码原则下的平均解码错误概率的显式公式，并推导了所有$(n,q^{Rn})_q$码的渐近错误指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究擦除信道下不同解码原则的平均解码错误概率和渐近错误指数，以评估编码性能。

Method: 通过数学推导提供平均解码错误概率的显式公式，并计算渐近错误指数。

Result: 获得了不同解码原则下的平均解码错误概率公式和渐近错误指数。

Conclusion: 为擦除信道下的编码性能评估提供了理论依据和计算工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide explicit formulas for the average decoding error probabilities of the ensemble of all $(n,M)_q$ codes over the erasure channel under unambiguous, list and maximum-likelihood decoding principles. Moreover, we derive the asymptotic error exponents for the ensemble of all $(n,q^{Rn})_q$ codes.

</details>


### [269] [Near-Field Physical-Layer Authentication Under Impersonation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04879)
*Hajar El Hassani,Linda Senigagliesi,Arsenia Chorti*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了近场条件下的物理层认证（PLA）在身份冒充攻击下的表现，指出近场中波束导向向量依赖于角度和距离，推导了单天线和多天线攻击者的最优预编码方案，并通过仿真验证了距离差异足以阻止成功的冒充攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的物理层认证研究主要集中在远场场景，其中导向向量仅依赖于到达角（AoA），而在近场场景中，导向向量同时依赖于角度和距离，因此需要研究近场条件下的身份冒充攻击特性。

Method: 通过最小化合法发射机Alice和主动攻击者Eve生成信号之间的均方误差（MSE）来分析攻击，推导单天线Eve的最优标量预编码器和多天线Eve的最优预编码向量，并在标准二阶菲涅尔近似下进行分析。

Result: 对于单天线Eve，只有当Eve与Alice具有相同的角度和距离时才能实现完美冒充；对于多天线Eve，只有当Alice的导向向量属于Eve导向向量张成的子空间时才能实现完美冒充；仿真结果表明，在近场中，即使AoA相同，距离差异也足以阻止成功的冒充攻击。

Conclusion: 近场条件下的物理层认证具有更强的安全性，距离差异可以作为区分合法用户和攻击者的有效特征，验证了近场PLA在防止身份冒充攻击方面的有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies physical-layer authentication (PLA) in the near-field regime under impersonation attacks. Unlike the far-field case, where the steering vector depends only on the angle of arrival (AoA), in the near field it depends on both angle and distance. We analyze the attack by minimizing the mean-square error (MSE) between the signal received from a legitimate transmitter Alice and the signal generated by an active attacker Eve. For a single-antenna Eve, we derive the optimal scalar precoder and show that, under the standard second-order Fresnel approximation, perfect impersonation is possible only if Eve has the same angle and the same distance from Bob as Alice. We then extend the analysis to a multi-antenna Eve and derive the optimal precoding vector. In this case, perfect impersonation is possible only if Alice steering vector belongs to the subspace spanned by Eve steering vectors. Simulation results show that, in the near field, a distance difference is sufficient to prevent a successful impersonation attack even when Alice and Eve have the same AoA, and confirm the analytical results.

</details>


### [270] [Minimum Schubert Codewords and Second-Minimum Grassmann Codewords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04916)
*Muskan Khaneja,Prasant Singh*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解决了 Ghorpade 和 Singh 在 2018 年提出的关于 Schubert 码最小权码字分类的猜想，并利用该分类证明了 Grassmann 码中第二最小权码字的充要条件，最后给出了第二最小权码字的计数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Ghorpade 和 Singh 于 2018 年提出的关于 Schubert 码 $C_α(\ell, m)$ 最小权码字分类的公开猜想，并进一步研究 Grassmann 码 $C(\ell, m)$ 的第二最小权码字特性。

Method: 通过分类 Schubert 码的最小权码字来验证猜想，利用该分类结果推导 Grassmann 码中第二最小权码字的代数结构特征（涉及外积空间中可分解向量与交替向量的乘积），并进行枚举计数。

Result: 1. 对所有 $q$ 和 $α$ 值完成了 Schubert 码最小权码字的分类，证实了 2018 年的猜想；2. 证明了 Grassmann 码码字具有第二最小权当且仅当其索引可写为可分解 $(m-\ell-2)$-向量与秩为 4 的交替 2-向量的乘积；3. 给出了 Grassmann 码第二最小权码字的具体枚举数量。

Conclusion: 该研究完整解决了 Schubert 码最小权码字的分类问题，并建立了 Grassmann 码第二最小权码字的精确代数刻画与计数公式，深化了对代数编码理论中几何码结构的理解。

Abstract: In this paper, we give a classification of the minimum weight codewords of Schubert codes $C_α(\ell, m)$ by settling the conjecture proposed by Ghorpade and Singh in 2018, for all values of $q$ and all $α$. We use this classification to prove that a codeword of the Grassmann code $C(\ell, m)$ has the second minimum weight if and only if it is indexed by an element of $\bigwedge^{m-\ell}V$ that can be written as the product of a decomposable $(m-\ell-2)$-vector and an alternating $2$-vector of rank $4$. Finally, we give an enumeration of the second minimum weight codewords of the Grassmann code.

</details>


### [271] [Algebraic Geometry Codes Approach the Half-Singleton Bound with Constant Field Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05017)
*Neehar Verma,Camilla Hollanti,Razane Tajeddine*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析评估码随机穿孔的通用框架，用于研究插入删除（insdel）错误下的线性码，证明了随机Reed-Solomon码和基于代数几何码的结构化线性码族可逼近半Singleton界，并优化了对加性间隙$\varepsilon$的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决插入删除错误下线性码的分析难题，特别是简化Con等人（ICALP 2025）关于最长公共子序列的复杂分析，并改进随机Reed-Solomon码逼近半Singleton界时对加性间隙$\varepsilon$的指数依赖关系。

Method: 开发了一个通用框架，通过分析评估码的随机穿孔，将编辑距离的控制简化为仅依赖于评估域的大小和底层函数空间中非零函数的最大零点数；该框架被实例化为随机穿孔的Reed-Muller码，并应用于由渐近好函数域塔产生的代数几何码。

Result: 1. 推广并简化了Con等人（ICALP 2025）的结果，避免了对最长公共子序列的深入分析；2. 证明了随机Reed-Solomon码在线性大小域上逼近半Singleton界，并将对$\varepsilon$的依赖从$2^{O(1/\varepsilon^2)}$优化至$2^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$；3. 构造了常数大小域上的随机结构化线性码族，使其能逼近半Singleton界。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用框架不仅统一并简化了现有理论分析，还显著提升了码的性能界限，证明了在常数大小域上存在逼近半Singleton界的结构化线性码族，为构造高效抗插入删除错误的编码方案提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study linear codes for insertion and deletion (insdel) errors through the lens of evaluation codes. We develop a general framework for analyzing random puncturings of evaluation codes, where the edit distance is controlled by only the size of the evaluation domain and the maximum number of zeros of a nonzero function in the underlying function space. Our proof generalizes the results of Con, Guo, Li, and Zhang (ICALP 2025), and simultaneously simplifies their arguments by avoiding an in-depth analysis of longest common subsequences. We demonstrate the applicability of our core theorem by instantiating it with random puncturings of Reed--Muller codes. We then recover the result that random Reed--Solomon codes approach the half-Singleton bound over linear-sized fields while also improving the dependence on the additive gap $\varepsilon$ from $2^{O(1/\varepsilon^2)}$ to $2^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$. Finally, by applying the framework to algebraic geometry codes arising from asymptotically good towers of function fields, we show that there exist randomized families of structured linear codes over constant-sized fields that approach the half-Singleton bound.

</details>


### [272] [Latency-Optimal Geo-Distributed Storage over Structured Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05229)
*Madhura Pathegama,Viveck Cadambe*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了地理分布式存储系统中的延迟最优文件分配问题，证明了对于固定文件数量（k≥3），该问题是NP难的；但同时发现树、特定环及高最小度单位权图等特定网络拓扑存在无需编码的结构化最优解，并提供了相应的高效构造算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在最小化地理分布式存储系统中检索原始文件的平均时间，解决在加权图模型（边权代表通信延迟）下如何最优分配文件（包括编码文件）以优化系统延迟的问题。

Method: 通过从支配集数问题（domatic number problem）进行归约来证明计算复杂性；通过分析特定网络拓扑（如加权树、特定加权环、单位权图）的结构特性，识别出存在非编码结构化最优分配的条件，并设计相应的高效算法。

Result: 1. 证明了对于任意固定文件数 k≥3，计算延迟最小化分配是 NP 难问题。2. 确定了加权树、特定加权环以及具有足够大最小度的单位权图 admits 无需编码的结构化最优分配（即每个节点可选择其最近的 k 个节点存储不同原始文件）。3. 为上述图类提供了构造延迟最优文件分配的高效算法。

Conclusion: 虽然通用的延迟最优文件分配问题在计算上是困难的，但在多种自然且常见的网络拓扑结构中存在高效的可解实例，允许通过简单的非编码结构化策略实现最优延迟。

Abstract: We study latency-optimal file assignment in geo-distributed storage systems modeled as weighted graphs, where edge weights represent communication delays and each node stores one (possibly coded) file. Our goal is to minimize the average time required to retrieve an original file, taken uniformly over all nodes and files. We show that for every fixed number of files $k \geq 3$, computing a latency-minimizing assignment is NP-hard via a reduction from the domatic number problem. On the positive side, we identify natural network topologies that admit uncoded, structured optimal assignments in which, for every node, one can choose its $k$ closest nodes, including itself, so that they store distinct original files. We prove that every weighted tree, certain weighted cycles, and unit-weight graphs with sufficiently large minimum degree admit such assignments. For these graph classes, we provide efficient algorithms to construct latency-optimal file assignments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [Spectral-Target Physical Latent Structuring for JEPA-Style World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04264)
*Penghao Zhu,Salvatore Penachio,Kaustav Mukherjee,Aneesh Jonelagadda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对潜在世界模型在动态环境中出现的“物理表征懒惰”问题，提出了一种轻量级的“傅里叶辅助头”训练方法，通过引入物理信息的辅助监督，显著提升了规划成功率和数据效率，且无额外推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有的正则化技术（如SIGReg）能防止表征坍塌，但在高度动态环境中，潜在世界模型仍会出现“物理表征懒惰”的新失效模式：潜在状态虽未坍塌，却未能捕捉关键物理属性，导致下游规划普遍失败。

Method: 提出在训练阶段引入轻量级的“傅里叶辅助头”进行辅助监督，强制潜在空间具备物理信息的结构化特征；该方法通用性强，适用于任何环境，且不增加推理时的计算开销。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在存在物理表征懒惰的动态环境中大幅提升了规划成功率；在其他环境中也有适度提升；低数据 regime 下效果尤为显著；同时观察到规划性能提升与潜在空间同关键物理属性的相关性增强呈正相关。

Conclusion: 傅里叶辅助头能有效避免潜在世界模型中的表征懒惰问题，通过物理结构化潜在状态，提升整体规划成功率和数据效率，具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Latent world models have become increasingly popular as a method to predict and plan in latent space rather than pixel space. Recent architectures, such as LeWorldModel (LeWM), jointly train the encoder and predictor using regularization techniques like SIGReg to prevent representation collapse. Even with such regularization preventing representation collapse, we identify a new world model failure mode of \textit{physical representation laziness}, particularly noted in highly dynamic environments. For these lazy cases, the learned latent states do not collapse but nonetheless fail to represent key physical properties, causing ubiquitous downstream planning failure. To resolve this issue, we propose training-time auxiliary supervision with a lightweight "Fourier auxiliary head", which enforces physically-informed structuring of the latent space with no additional inference-time cost and can be generalized to any environment. Experimentally, we show that the auxiliary head substantially improves planning success rates in dynamic environments where the baseline LeWM exhibits physical representation laziness. It also leads to modest improvements in other environments, even when the baseline does not exhibit physical representation laziness. We further observe superior planning performance being accompanied by higher latent space correlations with key physical properties, indicating both the ability of our method to physically structure latent states and the potential planning-side benefit to the learned representation being physically structured. We also see in low-data regimes, auxiliary supervision is particularly impactful in increasing success rate. These findings support the use of our Fourier auxiliary head method to improve both overall success rate and data efficiency, while avoiding representation laziness in latent world models.

</details>


### [274] [ProToMEx: Rapid, Interpretable Explanations via Structured Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04265)
*Athina Georgara,Adarsh Valoor,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProToMEx，一种利用概率主题模型（PTMs）的新型可解释性框架，旨在通过发现潜在的“主题”来揭示驱动分类决策的复杂组合模式，而非仅仅提供特征重要性评分。该方法在保持与SHAP和LIME相当的解释忠实度的同时，将局部解释的生成速度提升了30-40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的事后解释器主要关注特征归因（分配重要性分数），难以阐明驱动模型决策的复杂组合模式和高阶语义结构。

Method: 引入ProToMEx框架，利用模型无关的概率主题模型（PTMs）学习潜在“主题”，这些主题代表分类的不同高层级原因，从而同时提供全局行为解释和能够解耦特定预测中多重共存原因的局部解释。

Result: 实验表明，ProToMEx生成的解释在忠实度上与SHAP和LIME相当，但在标准化表格数据集和合成数据集上，其生成局部解释的摊销计算成本显著降低，速度比SHAP和LIME快约30-40倍。

Conclusion: ProToMEx通过揭示底层语义结构克服了传统特征归因的局限性，并因其极高的计算效率，特别适用于需要实时解释的应用场景。

Abstract: Existing post-hoc explainers for machine learning classifiers primarily focus on feature attribution, assigning importance scores to individual features. While valuable, this approach struggles to articulate the complex, combinatorial patterns that often drive a model's decision-making process. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProToMEx, a new paradigm for explainability that leverages Probabilistic Topic Models (PTMs). Our model-agnostic framework learns latent ''topics'' that represent distinct, high-level reasons for a classification, moving beyond simple feature importance to reveal underlying semantic structures. ProToMEx naturally provides both global explanations of a model's overall behaviour and local explanations that can disentangle multiple co-existing reasons for a specific prediction. We demonstrate empirically that ProToMEx not only produces explanations of comparable fidelity to popular methods like SHAP and LIME but also drastically reduces the amortised computational cost of generating local explanations, making it highly suitable for real-time applications. Specifically, we show that ProToMEx is ~30-40x faster than SHAP and LIME over standardised tabular datasets and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [275] [A Data Fusion Framework for Grounding Aerospace Surrogate Model via Experimental Wind-Tunnel Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04267)
*Nitin Nagesh Kulkarni,Dheeraj Vemula,Yin Yu,Peter Lyu,Juan J. Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实验数据的校正框架，利用风洞PSP测量数据对基于高保真CFD训练的深度学代理模型进行修正，显著提升了气动预测与实验观测的一致性，同时保留了模型的泛化能力和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于高保真CFD数据训练的气动代理模型能准确复现数值预测，但其预测精度受限于CFD模拟与实验观测之间存在的系统性偏差，导致模型无法直接匹配真实实验数据。

Method: 采用在2,300个NASA CRM翼身组合体高保真CFD仿真数据上训练的Geotransolver代理模型，并引入一个校正网络。该校正网络利用两个自由流马赫数（0.70和0.85）下的空间注册PSP测量数据进行训练，学习代理模型预测与实验测量表面压力分布之间的差异，从而在不修改预训练代理模型参数的前提下实现实验信息的融合。

Result: 在马赫数0.85条件下，校正显著改善了与PSP数据的一致性，特别是在翼型吸力峰值、激波位置及后续压力恢复区域，降低了预测误差的幅值及误差超过0.05 Cp的湿润表面比例。在预留的攻角测试中，校正后的代理模型与测量值的偏差控制在测量Cp范围的2.3%-2.7%以内，且性能优于直接插值方法。

Conclusion: 实验测量数据可以通过学习CFD到实验的系统性偏差来“锚定”大规模仿真训练的代理模型，在保持其泛化能力和计算效率的同时，显著提升预测的物理真实性。

Abstract: Aerodynamic surrogate models trained on high-fidelity CFD data reproduce numerical predictions of both scalar outputs and entire fields accurately, yet their predictive fidelity is limited by systematic discrepancies between CFD and experimental observations. We present an experimentally grounded correction framework that adapts a CFD-trained deep learning surrogate using wind-tunnel PSP measurements. A Geotransolver surrogate trained on 2,300 high-fidelity CFD simulations of the NASA CRM wing-body configuration, spanning geometric variation, Mach 0.70-0.85, and angles of attack 0 to 4 degrees, reproduces the CFD integrated aerodynamic forces and pitching moment to R2 > 0.99 but does not match the experimental data. To incorporate experimental information without retraining the surrogate, a correction network is trained on spatially registered PSP measurements at two freestream Mach numbers (0.70 and 0.85) across the same angle-of-attack range, learning the discrepancy between the surrogate-predicted and experimentally measured surface-pressure distributions. At Mach 0.85 the correction substantially improves agreement with PSP, particularly at the wing suction peak, shock location, and subsequent pressure recovery, reducing both the magnitude of the prediction error and the fraction of wetted surface on which it exceeds 0.05 in Cp, and it does so from a limited experimental dataset without modifying the pretrained surrogate parameters. On held-out angles of attack the grounded surrogate agrees with measurement to within 2.3-2.7% of the measured Cp range, and outperforms direct interpolation between the measured conditions at every state tested. Experimental measurements can therefore ground a large-scale simulation-trained surrogate by learning systematic CFD-to-experiment discrepancies while preserving its generalization capability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [276] [Quantum-Assisted Memory-Efficient Training for Parameter-Intensive Wi-Fi-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04271)
*To Truong An,Jie Zhang,Guolin Yin,Junqing Zhang,Yanjiao Li,Trung Q. Duong,Simon L. Cotton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子辅助内存高效训练框架（Q-MET），用于Wi-Fi人类活动识别（HAR），通过混合量子经典神经网络间接生成参数并结合结构化剪枝，大幅减少训练参数量和推理资源消耗，同时保持高分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Wi-Fi HAR系统依赖深度学习模型，训练和推理过程计算和内存开销大，难以在资源受限设备上部署；传统训练需同时更新数百万参数，内存消耗过高。

Method: 提出Q-MET框架，采用混合量子经典神经网络间接生成HAR模型参数以减少可训练参数数量，并在训练阶段集成结构化剪枝以支持轻量级推理。

Result: 相比传统反向传播深度学习训练，Q-MET减少90%-95%可训练参数，分类准确率持平或更高；结构化剪枝实现75%-85%模型稀疏度，分类准确率损失小于2%。

Conclusion: Q-MET是首个同时解决HAR系统训练和推理阶段内存低效问题的量子辅助方法，为资源受限设备上的Wi-Fi HAR部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Wi-Fi-based human activity recognition (HAR) has become an important part of integrated sensing and communications, paving the way for a range of context-aware services. However, most existing Wi-Fi-based HAR systems rely on deep learning (DL) models that are computationally and memory intensive in both training and inference, which poses significant challenges for real-world deployment. Conventional training requires simultaneous updates of millions of parameters, leading to prohibitive memory consumption. In this paper, we propose a novel quantum-assisted memory-efficient training framework (Q-MET) designed to improve efficiency in both training and inference. Q-MET utilizes a hybrid quantum classical neural network to indirectly generate parameters for HAR models, significantly reducing the trainable parameter count compared to direct optimization. To further support the deployment on resource-constrained devices, we integrate structured pruning during the training phase. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-MET achieves a 90% to 95% reduction in trainable parameters compared with conventional backpropagation-based DL training while maintaining or even exceeding classical classification accuracy. Additionally, Q-MET supports lightweight inference through structured pruning, achieving 75% to 85% model sparsity with less than 2% loss in classification accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this work represents the first quantum-assisted approach to simultaneously tackle memory inefficiencies in both the training and inference stages of HAR systems.

</details>


### [277] [Interpretability for Turing Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04661)
*Billy Snikkers,Rumi Salazar,Daniel Murfet,Will Troiani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明神经网络可解释性技术“敏感性”可识别图灵机中的算法结构，并通过主成分分析和聚类在敏感性空间中恢复算法特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索神经网络可解释性技术（如敏感性）是否能用于理解和分析传统计算模型（如图灵机）中的算法结构，建立深度学习可解释性与经典计算理论之间的联系。

Method: 通过探测噪声图灵机学习问题的局部损失景观，证明图灵机算法中的对称性和路径分离会导致其敏感性矩阵中出现置换对称性和低秩块；在确定性有限自动机（DFA）集合上进行实证研究，使用主成分分析和聚类方法分析敏感性空间。

Result: 证明了算法对称性和路径分离会在敏感性矩阵中产生特定的数学结构（置换对称性和低秩块），并在DFA实验中成功通过PCA和聚类恢复了算法特征。

Conclusion: 敏感性技术可有效识别图灵机中的算法结构，为连接神经网络可解释性与经典计算理论提供了新方法和实证支持。

Abstract: We show that susceptibilities, an interpretability technique developed for neural networks, can identify the presence of algorithmic structure in Turing machines by probing the local loss landscape of a learning problem for noisy Turing machines introduced by Murfet and Troiani (arXiv:2504.08075). We prove that symmetries and path separation in the algorithm implemented by a Turing machine induce permutation symmetries and low-rank blocks in its susceptibility matrix. We study this empirically on a set of deterministic finite automata (DFAs) and demonstrate that algorithmic features can be recovered by principal component analysis and clustering methods in susceptibility space.

</details>


### [278] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Forced Outage Risk Prediction: Benefits and Comparison to Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04272)
*Christos Petridis,Zoran Obradovic,Mladen Kezunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在零样本框架下预测配电网络中天气相关强制停电风险的能力，并与监督学习模型进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLMs在无需标注训练数据的情况下，能否有效预测电网因天气导致的停电风险，以评估其在电力系统风险管理中的潜力。

Method: 将问题构建为三个预测时间范围（3小时、6小时、12小时）的二元严重性分类任务，使用德克萨斯州中部六年停电记录与高分辨率天气数据，对比四种零样本LLMs与两种监督分类器在两种输入配置（当前天气观测与天气预报数据）下的表现。

Result: 监督模型在宏观F1分数和精确度上优于LLMs，但新一代LLMs表现具有竞争力；LLMs在可操作推理和地理可扩展性方面展现出互补优势。

Conclusion: 结合LLMs与监督模型可能是最佳实践，以兼顾预测准确性与推理及扩展能力。

Abstract: This study examines the ability of large language models (LLMs) to predict the risk of weather-related forced outages in the distribution grid in a zero-shot framework, without labeled training data. The problem is formulated as a binary severity classification task across three forecast horizons (3h, 6h, 12h), using six years of outage records and high-resolution weather data for a utility service area in central Texas. Four zero-shot LLMs are benchmarked against two supervised classifiers across two input configurations: one using current weather observations and the other using weather forecast data. Results show that supervised models outperform LLMs on macro-F1 and precision, while newer LLM generations achieve competitive scores. Beyond accuracy, LLMs offer complementary strengths in actionable reasoning and geographic scalability, suggesting that combining them with supervised models may be the best practice.

</details>


### [279] [BER-PEF: Unified Human Mobility Predictability Evaluation via Bayes Error Rate Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04292)
*En Xu,Jingtao Ding,Zhiwen Yu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BER-PEF 框架，基于贝叶斯错误率将预测误差估计转化为移动可预测性估计，在无可观测真实值情况下统一评估不同估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动可预测性的真实值在现实数据中不可直接观测，导致缺乏统一标准来比较不同估计方法的性能。

Method: 构建基于贝叶斯错误率（BER）的框架 BER-PEF，将符号序列、数值轨迹、上下文特征和学得的表示映射到统一的特征 - 标签空间，通过控制扰动曲线评估估计器输出与共享可预测性参考区间的偏差。

Result: 在 Foursquare NYC/TKY、GeoLife 和 T-Drive 数据集上，多个 BER 基础估计器在符号序列和数值轨迹上的参考偏差低于现有方法，且其估计值能跟踪扰动下经验预测性能的变化；上下文输入和多结构表示可在同一协议下评估，多扰动层级聚合证据比单一未扰动观测更可靠。

Conclusion: BER-PEF 为在真实可预测性不可用时，对异构移动数据上的可预测性估计器提供了一条统一且可验证的评估路径。

Abstract: Human mobility predictability concerns the best prediction performance attainable from a given target and input information, but its ground truth is not directly observable on real mobility data. We present BER-PEF, a Bayes-error-rate-based framework that converts BER estimation into mobility predictability estimation and provides a unified protocol for comparing estimators without observable ground truth. The framework maps symbolic sequences, numeric trajectories, contextual features, and learned representations into a common feature--label space, then evaluates estimator outputs along controlled perturbation curves against a shared predictability reference interval by measuring deviations below the interval, above the interval, and across the full interval. Experiments on Foursquare NYC and TKY, GeoLife, and T-Drive show that several BER-based estimators achieve lower reference discrepancy than existing predictability methods on symbolic sequences and numeric trajectories, while their estimates track changes in empirical prediction performance under perturbation. Additional analyses show that contextual inputs and multiple structured representations can be evaluated under the same protocol, and that aggregating evidence across multiple perturbation levels provides a more reliable basis for estimator selection than relying on a single unperturbed observation. BER-PEF therefore offers a unified and verifiable path for evaluating predictability estimators on heterogeneous mobility data when ground-truth predictability is unavailable.

</details>


### [280] [Confounding-Valid Conformal Inference for Counterfactual KPIs in Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05073)
*Abdessamed Qchohi,Jessica Moysen Cortes,Matteo Zecchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CV-CCI方法，通过结合大量观测遥测数据与少量随机化数据，解决了网络反事实推断中因隐藏混杂导致的统计保证失效问题，在确保有效性的同时提升了预测集的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营商利用日志遥测数据进行反事实推断（回答“假设”问题）时，常因日志遗漏控制器使用的变量而产生隐藏混杂，导致统计保证失效。虽然随机化遥测数据可解决此问题，但其采集可能干扰正常网络运行，导致数据稀缺且单独使用时预测集信息量不足。

Method: 提出“混杂有效反事实共形推断”（CV-CCI），基于广义合成赋能推断（GESPI）原则，将丰富但可能存在混杂的观测遥测数据与有限但无混杂的随机化数据相结合。该方法利用观测数据提高推断效率，同时依赖随机化数据在任意隐藏混杂下保持有限样本覆盖率保证。

Result: 在两个代表性的无线接入网（RAN）控制任务实验中，CV-CCI在存在隐藏混杂的情况下仍保持统计有效性，且生成的预测集比现有的混杂有效基线方法更高效（即预测集更紧凑、信息量更大）。

Conclusion: CV-CCI成功平衡了数据效率与统计鲁棒性，为网络运营中利用非理想日志数据进行可靠的反事实分析提供了有效解决方案，克服了单纯依赖稀缺随机化数据或忽略混杂效应的局限性。

Abstract: Conformal counterfactual inference enables network operators to use logged telemetry to reliably answer 'what-if' questions about network operation. These answers typically take the form of prediction sets that contain, with a user-defined probability, the key performance indicators (KPIs) that would have been observed under alternative control actions. A key challenge is that logged telemetry may omit variables used by the controller, resulting in hidden confounding and invalidating the statistical guarantees of counterfactual analysis. In principle, this issue can be addressed using randomized telemetry, collected by assigning control actions independently of the network state. However, because such randomization may disrupt normal operation, randomized telemetry is typically scarce, causing counterfactual analysis based solely on it to produce uninformative prediction sets. To address these challenges, we propose Confounding-Valid Counterfactual Conformal Inference (CV-CCI), which combines abundant, potentially confounded observational telemetry with limited randomized data through the General Synthetic-Powered Inference (GESPI) principle. CV-CCI leverages observational data to improve efficiency while using randomized data to retain finite-sample coverage guarantees under arbitrary hidden confounding. Experiments on two representative radio access network (RAN) control tasks show that CV-CCI remains valid under hidden confounding while producing more efficient prediction sets than state-of-the-art confounding-valid baselines.

</details>


### [281] [Learning from VAE Errors to support ECG-based Differential Diagnosis of Myocardial Scar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05294)
*Shayan Sharifi,Riccardo Treu,Ilaria Gandin,Federico Garoia,Marco Merlo,Giulia Cisotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于β-变分自编码器（β-VAE）的ECG表征能否区分LGE+和LGE-心肌病患者，发现ECGx.AI模型表现优于浅层β-VAE，但DTW重构误差可作为瘢痕相关ECG改变的潜在标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏磁共振上的延迟钆增强（LGE）是心肌瘢痕的关键标志物，但其可及性有限，因此需要基于常规ECG的筛查方法。

Method: 在300名受试者的本地队列中，比较了来自基础ECGx.AI模型的32维特征与在正常PTB-XL ECG上训练的浅层β-VAE的特征，评估下游分类性能和基于动态时间规整（DTW）的重构误差。

Result: ECGx.AI结合随机森林的ROC曲线下面积为0.686，而β-VAE结合梯度提升的AUC为0.577（敏感度0.775）；DTW重构误差在12导联中的10个导联上类别间差异显著，结合逻辑回归后AUC提升至0.643。

Conclusion: β-VAE衍生的ECG表征在区分LGE状态方面表现有限，但DTW重构误差显示出作为瘢痕相关ECG改变标志物的潜力，支持其在ECG筛查中的应用前景。

Abstract: Late Gadolinium Enhancement (LGE) on cardiac magnetic resonance is a key marker of myocardial scar, but its limited accessibility motivates routine ECG-based screening. We evaluated whether $β$-variational autoencoder (VAE)-derived ECG representations can discriminate LGE+ from LGE- cardiomyopathic patients in a local cohort of 300 subjects. We compared 32-dimensional features from the foundation ECGx.AI model with those from a shallower $β$-VAE trained on normal PTB-XL ECGs, evaluating downstream classification and Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based reconstruction errors. ECGx.AI reached an area under ROC of 0.686 with Random Forest, while the proposed $β$-VAE reached 0.577 with sensitivity of 0.775 with Gradient Boosting. Notably, DTW-reconstruction errors significantly differed between classes in 10 out of 12 leads according to Mann-Whitney U test and help in classification, leading to an area under ROC of 0.643 with Logistic Regression, supporting their potential as markers of scar-related ECG alterations.

</details>


### [282] [Data-Driven Learning of Unknown Nonlinear Differential Equations Using Functional Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04329)
*Seyyed Shaho Alaviani,Yongzhi Qu,Gregory W. Vogl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的可解释机器学习方法，用于从单一状态轨迹数据中发现非线性常微分方程的未知向量场，无需先验物理知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动发现非线性常微分方程的方法存在局限性，需要改进以更好地处理未知系统动力学和外部力的发现。

Method: 基于泛函分析和算子理论构建公式，在函数空间中构造成本函数作为两个函数之间的积分距离，并提出增量学习算法以在线处理新训练样本。

Result: 该方法能够同时发现未知外力（作为时间函数）和未知底层动力学，适用于受迫和非受迫的自治及非自治动态系统。

Conclusion: 数值示例证明了所提出方法的优势，展示了其在数据驱动发现非线性常微分方程方面的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, the problem of data-driven discovery of nonlinear ordinary differential equations (ODEs) is recast, and a new interpretable machine learning (ML) method is proposed.
