TL;DR: 本文研究了节点到达流模型中相似图上的多个统计问题，开发了常数遍、次线性空间的流算法，并建立了与上界在流长度依赖性上几乎匹配的空间下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在节点到达流模型中处理相似图上的统计问题（如度矩、多样性指数等）具有挑战性，需要高效的流算法来应对大规模数据流。

Method: 开发了常数遍、次线性空间的流算法来解决度矩、多样性指数、度矩采样和多样性采样等问题，并建立了相应的空间下界。

Result: 成功设计了针对相似图上多个统计问题的流算法，其空间复杂度为次线性，且空间下界与上界在流长度依赖性上几乎匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究为节点到达流模型中相似图的统计问题提供了高效的算法解决方案，并在空间复杂度上达到了理论上的最优或接近最优。

Abstract: In this paper, we study several statistical problems on similarity graphs in the node-arrival streaming model, including degree moments, diversity index, degree-moment sampling, and diversity sampling. We develop constant-pass, sublinear-space streaming algorithms for these problems and establish space lower bounds that nearly match the upper bounds in their dependence on the stream length.

</details>


### [357] [A Polymatroidal Perspective on Random Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04521)
*Karthekeyan Chandrasekaran,Chandra Chekuri,Weihao Zhu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的算法框架，用于研究拟阵商的最小化问题，将现有的超图和 hedge 图的最小割算法纳入该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了统一理解现有的多种非均匀随机收缩算法，特别是针对超图和 hedge 图的最小割问题。

Method: 引入“商有界拟阵”（quotient-bounded polymatroids）的概念，并基于此构建通用的随机收缩算法框架。

Result: 证明了多个现有结果可以在商有界拟阵的统一算法框架下被推导和理解。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解随机收缩算法提供了统一的理论视角，扩展了最小割算法在更广泛结构上的应用。

Abstract: Karger's elegant random contraction algorithm for finding a global mincut in a graph has been highly influential. More recent work has obtained several different (nonuniform) random contraction algorithms for mincut in hypergraphs and hedgegraphs. Motivated by the conceptual goal of understanding these algorithms in a unified fashion, we study random contraction algorithms for finding a minimum quotient of a polymatroid. We introduce the notion of quotient-bounded polymatroids and show that several existing results can be derived and understood under a common algorithmic framework for quotient-bounded polymatroids.

</details>


### [358] [On Maximizing a Weakly Submodular Function over a Matroid Constraint via the Greedy Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05008)
*Justin Ward,Moran Feldman*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了在一般拟阵约束下使用标准贪心算法近似最大化弱次模函数的问题，发现该算法无法提供常数近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贪心算法在基数约束下对弱次模函数最大化问题能给出紧的近似结果，但在一般拟阵约束下的表现尚不明确，需要探究其近似性能。

Method: 构造了一族γ-弱次模函数和简单的划分拟阵约束，通过理论分析证明标准贪心算法在该约束下的近似性能。

Result: 对于任何γ<1，标准贪心算法在划分拟阵约束下无法为弱次模函数最大化问题提供常数近似比。

Conclusion: 标准贪心算法在一般拟阵约束下对弱次模函数最大化问题不适用，需要寻找其他算法或约束条件来获得更好的近似结果。

Abstract: We consider the problem of approximately maximizing a weakly submodular function using the standard greedy algorithm, which is known to give tight approximation results for such functions under a cardinality constraint. We show that this is not the case for general matroid constraints. For any $γ< 1$, we give a family of $γ$-weakly submodular functions and a simple partition matroid constraint and show that the standard greedy algorithm provides no constant approximation for the resulting constrained maximization problem.

</details>


### [359] [Online Matching in Convex Bipartite Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05057)
*Yilong Feng,Zhihao Gavin Tang,Kangning Wang,Xiaowei Wu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了凸二分图上的在线匹配问题，证明了仅凭凸性无法提升经典的1-1/e竞争比；但在请求具有统一长度（d个连续邻居）的模型下，提出的随机算法Flip通过混合“最早可行”和“最晚可行”策略，实现了2/3的竞争比，且该结果是紧的，同时证明了该模型下随机算法的上界为3/4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在线资源分配系统（如门诊预约、频谱分配）中，顺序到达的请求需分配连续资源区间的问题，探究在不可撤销决策和对抗性到达模式下，凸性结构是否能优化匹配效率。

Method: 首先分析凸二分图的一般情况；随后聚焦于统一长度模型，提出Flip算法，该算法利用一个随机比特位，在事前决定采用“最早可行分配”或“最晚可行分配”策略，通过随机化混合两种确定性策略来避免容量浪费。

Result: 1. 凸性本身不能改善Ranking算法的1-1/e最坏情况保证；2. 在统一长度模型中，Flip算法实现了2/3的竞争比，且该界是紧的，即使面对半自适应对手依然有效；3. 证明了在该模型下，任何随机在线算法的竞争比无法严格超过3/4。

Conclusion: 虽然凸性结构在一般情况下无助于提升竞争比，但在具有统一长度约束的特定场景下，简单的随机化混合策略（Flip）能显著优于单一确定性策略，将竞争比从1/2提升至2/3，但该模型存在3/4的理论性能上限。

Abstract: Online resource-allocation systems, like outpatient scheduling and spectrum allocation, often assign sequentially arriving requests to an ordered pool of scarce resources, where each request accepts a contiguous interval of feasible options. We study the resulting online matching problem on convex bipartite graphs under irrevocable decisions and adversarial arrivals. We first show that convexity alone does not improve the classic worst-case guarantee of 1-1/e, achieved by Ranking. We then consider the uniform-length model, in which every online request has exactly d consecutive offline neighbors. We propose Flip, which uses one random bit to commit ex-ante to either earliest-feasible assignment or latest-feasible assignment. Although either natural deterministic policy can waste capacity and be asymptotically only 1/2-competitive, we show that their randomized mixture is 2/3-competitive. This guarantee is tight for Flip and remains valid against a semi-adaptive adversary that observes the selected policy before choosing the arrival order. We also prove that no randomized online algorithm can achieve a competitive ratio strictly larger than 3/4 in the uniform-length model.

</details>
